He was utterly alone on the headland, and his allies had vanished into space, while the enemy plans, moving like clockwork, were approaching their consummation. For a second he thought of leaving the Tower and hiding somewhere in the cliffs. He dismissed the notion unwillingly, for he remembered the task that had been set him. He was there to hold the fort to the last﻿—to gain time, though he could not for the life of him see what use time was to be when all the strategy of his own side seemed to have miscarried. Anyhow, the blackguards would be sold for they would not find the Princess. But he felt a horrid void in the pit of his stomach, and a looseness about his knees.

===Chinese===
这一来，赫里蒂奇彻底醒悟到自己处境的极端不妙。海岬上只他孤身一人，盟友尽数遁入虚空，而敌人的计划像钟表一样精确运转，眼看着就要大功告成。有一秒钟，他想弃塔而走，去崖壁间找个地方藏身。他不情愿地打消了这个念头，因为记起了派给他的任务。他守在这里，就是要撑到最后一刻——争的是时间，虽说他打死也想不出，自家这一方的方略分明已全线落空，时间还有什么用处。不管怎样，那帮恶棍终归要被耍一道，因为他们寻不见公主。可他只觉得胃底下空落落地发慌，两个膝盖也一阵阵发软。

===Original===
The moments passed more quickly as he wrestled with his fears. The next he knew the empty space below his window was filling with figures. There was a great crowd of them, rough fellows with seamen’s coats, still dripping as if they had had a wet landing. Dobson was with them, but for the rest they were strange figures.

===Chinese===
他同恐惧角力的当口，光阴倒过得快了些。再一留神，窗下那片空地里已经满满都是人影。黑压压一大群，全是穿水手外套的粗豪汉子，身上还在滴水，像是挨了一场湿淋淋的登陆。多布森混在其中，其余的面孔则一概陌生。

===Original===
Now that the expected had come at last Heritage’s nerves grew calmer. He made out that the newcomers were trying the door, and he waited to hear it fall, for such a mob could soon force it. But instead a voice called from beneath.

===Chinese===
盼头既然终于来了，赫里蒂奇的神经反倒镇静下来。他辨出那些新来的人在打门的主意，便等着听门倒下来的声响，这样一伙人，转眼就能破门。可底下却传来了唤话的声音。

===Original===
“Will you please open to us?” it said.

===Chinese===
“劳驾，请给我们开门。”那声音说。

===Original===
He stuck his head out and saw a little group with one man at the head of it, a young man clad in oilskins whose face was dim in the murky evening. The voice was that of a gentleman.

===Chinese===
他探出头去，望见底下一小簇人，为首的是个穿油布雨衣的青年，昏晦的暮色里面目模糊。那嗓音，是位绅士的嗓音。

===Original===
“I have orders to open to no one,” Heritage replied.

===Chinese===
“我奉命不给任何人开门。”赫里蒂奇答道。

===Original===
“Then I fear we must force an entrance,” said the voice.

===Chinese===
“那么恕我直言，我们只好破门而入了。”那声音说。

===Original===
“You can go to the devil,” said Heritage.

===Chinese===
“你见鬼去吧。”赫里蒂奇说。

===Original===
That defiance was the screw which his nerves needed. His temper had risen, he had forgotten all about the Princess, he did not even remember his isolation. His job was to make a fight for it. He ran up the staircase which led to the attics of the Tower, for he recollected that there was a window there which looked over the ground before the door. The place was ruinous, the floor filled with holes, and a part of the roof sagged down in a corner. The stones around the window were loose and crumbling and he managed to pull several out so that the slit was enlarged. He found himself looking down on a crowd of men, who had lifted the fallen tree on which Léon had perched, and were about to use it as a battering ram.

===Chinese===
这一记顶撞，恰好把他神经上需要的那颗螺丝拧紧了。他的火气上来了，把公主忘了个干净，连自己的孤绝处境也不记得了。他的差事，就是放手打一场。他奔上通往塔顶阁楼的楼梯，因为他记起那里有一扇窗，正俯瞰着门前那片地。阁楼破败不堪，地板上满是窟窿，一角的屋顶塌陷下来。窗口四周的石块酥松剥落，他抠出几块，把那道窄缝拓宽了。他俯瞰着底下的一群人：他们已抬起莱昂先前蹲踞的那棵倒树，正要拿它当撞门锤使。

===Original===
“The first fellow who comes within six yards of the door I shoot,” he shouted.

===Chinese===
“头一个凑到离门六码之内的，我就开枪。”他喊道。

===Original===
There was a white wave below as every face was turned to him. He ducked back his head in time as a bullet chipped the side of the window.

===Chinese===
底下泛起一片白色的浪头，一张张脸全朝他转了过来。一颗子弹削在窗口边沿，他及时缩回了脑袋。

===Original===
But his position was a good one, for he had a hole in the broken wall through which he could see, and could shoot with his hand at the edge of the window while keeping his body in cover. The battering party resumed their task, and as the tree swung nearer, he fired at the foremost of them. He missed, but the shot for a moment suspended operations.

===Chinese===
但他的位置很占便宜：破墙上有个洞眼，他可以由此瞭望，又能把手搭在窗口边沿开枪，身子却始终躲在掩体后面。撞门的那伙人重新干起活来，树干荡得近了，他朝最前面的一个开了枪。没打中，可这一枪叫那边的活计停顿了片刻。

===Original===
Again they came on, and again he fired. This time he damaged somebody, for the trunk was dropped.

===Chinese===
他们又涌上来，他又开了一枪。这回打伤了什么人，树干被撂下了。

===Original===
A voice gave orders, a sharp authoritative voice. The battering squad dissolved, and there was a general withdrawal out of the line of fire from the window. Was it possible that he had intimidated them? He could hear the sound of voices, and then a single figure came into sight again, holding something in its hand.

===Chinese===
一个声音发布号令，干脆而威严的声音。撞门小队登时散了，众人齐齐撤出窗口的射界。莫非他真把他们镇住了？他听得见人声嘈杂，随后一个孤零零的身影重新进入视野，手里握着个什么东西。

===Original===
He did not fire, for he recognised the futility of his efforts. The baseball swing of the figure below could not be mistaken. There was a roar beneath, and a flash of fire, as the bomb exploded on the door. Then came a rush of men, and the Tower had fallen.

===Chinese===
他没有开枪，因为他明白自己这些努力全是白费。底下那人抡臂的姿势像投棒球，绝不会认错。底下轰然一声，火光一闪，炸弹在门上炸开了。跟着人潮涌进，塔陷落了。

===Original===
Heritage clambered through a hole in the roof and gained the topmost parapet. He had still a pocketful of cartridges, and there in a coign of the old battlements he would prove an ugly customer to the pursuit. Only one at a time could reach that siege perilous.﻿ ﻿… They would not take long to search the lower rooms, and then would be hot on the trail of the man who had fooled them. He had not a scrap of fear left or even of anger﻿—only triumph at the thought of how properly those ruffians had been sold. “Like schoolboys they who unaware”﻿—instead of two women they had found a man with a gun. And the Princess was miles off and forever beyond their reach. When they had settled with him they would no doubt burn the House down, but that would serve them little. From his airy pinnacle he could see the whole seafront of Huntingtower, a blur in the dusk but for the ghostly eyes of its white-shuttered windows.

===Chinese===
赫里蒂奇从屋顶的一个破洞攀出去，登上了最高的垛墙。他口袋里还有满满一把子弹，在那古城垛的一角，他要让追兵领教一个何等难缠的主儿。那处险地，一次只容得一人攻上来。……他们搜遍下面几层用不了多久，随即就会紧紧咬住这个耍了他们的男人的踪迹。他心里没有一丝恐惧，连怒气也没有了——只剩一股得胜的欢喜，想着那帮恶棍被耍得这样地道。“正应了那句诗——‘浑然不觉的学童们’”——他们没找到两个女人，找到的是一个带枪的汉子。而公主远在数英里之外，永远出了他们的掌握。他们把他打发了之后，多半会放火烧了大宅，可那于他们没多少用处。从他这凌空的绝顶望下去，亨廷塔临海的整个正面尽收眼底，暮色里一团模糊，唯有那些上了白色窗板的窗洞，像一双双幽幽的鬼眼。

===Original===
Something was coming from it, running lightly over the lawns, lost for an instant in the trees, and then appearing clear on the crest of the ridge where some hours earlier Dougal had lain. With horror he saw that it was a girl. She stood with the wind plucking at her skirts and hair, and she cried in a high, clear voice which pierced even the confusion of the gale. What she cried he could not tell for it was in a strange tongue.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
有个身影正从宅子里出来，轻盈地掠过草坪，在树影里隐没了一瞬，又在山脊的脊线上清晰地现身——几个钟头前，杜加尔趴卧的正是那里。他惊恐地看出，那是个姑娘。她站在那里，风撕扯着她的裙裾和头发，她用一种高亢清亮的嗓音呼喊，那声音竟穿透了风暴的喧嚣。喊的是什么，他听不出来，因为那是一种陌生的语言。……

===Original===
But it reached the besiegers. There was a sudden silence in the din below him and then a confusion of shouting. The men seemed to be pouring out of the gap which had been the doorway, and as he peered over the parapet first one and then another entered his area of vision. The girl on the ridge, as soon as she saw that she had attracted attention, turned and ran back, and after her up the slopes went the pursuit bunched like hounds on a good scent.

===Chinese===
可那喊声传到了围攻者耳中。他脚下的喧嚣里蓦地一静，跟着是一片乱糟糟的喊叫。那些人像是从原先门洞的豁口里倒出来一般，他从垛口探望下去，一个接一个的人影闯进他的视野。山脊上的姑娘一见引来了注意，转身便跑，追兵在她身后涌上山坡，扎成一团，好似循着热腥的一群猎犬。

===Original===
Mr. John Heritage, swearing terribly, started to retrace his steps.

===Chinese===
约翰·赫里蒂奇先生一路狠狠咒骂着，动身往回赶。

## XIV. The Second Battle of the Cruives / 十四、克鲁夫斯第二战
===Original===
The military historian must often make shift to write of battles with slender data, but he can pad out his deficiencies by learned parallels. If his were the talented pen describing this, the latest action fought on British soil against a foreign foe, he would no doubt be crippled by the absence of written orders and war diaries. But how eloquently he would discant on the resemblance between Dougal and Gouraud﻿—how the plan of leaving the enemy to waste his strength upon a deserted position was that which on the 15th of July, 1918, the French general had used with decisive effect in Champagne! But Dougal had never heard of Gouraud, and I cannot claim that, like the Happy Warrior, he

===Chinese===
军事史家常常不得不凭着单薄的史料去写战事，好在还能搬出渊博的战例类比，把自家的窘处填塞起来。倘若执笔记述这一战——英伦本土上对外国来犯之敌打响的最近一战——的是他那支生花妙笔，他势必因缺少成文的命令和战地日志而缚手缚脚。但他定会何等雄辩地铺陈杜加尔与古罗（Gouraud）的相似之处——任凭敌人把力气虚耗在一座空阵地上，此一方略，正是那位法国将军在香巴尼（Champagne）以决定性之效施展过的——1918，7月15日！可惜杜加尔从未听说过古罗，而我也不能夸口说，他像那快乐的战士（the Happy Warrior）一样

===Original===
“through the heat of conflict kept the law
					

===Chinese===
“在鏖战的炽热中，仍谨守着

===Original===
					In calmness made, and saw what he foresaw.”

===Chinese===
于平静中立下的法度，并且望见了他所预见的景象。”

===Original===
I have had the benefit of discussing the affair with him and his colleagues, but I should offend against historic truth if I represented the main action as anything but a scrimmage﻿—a “soldiers’ battle,” the historian would say, a Malplaquet, an Albuera.

===Chinese===
我曾有幸同他及其同僚们讨论过这一战，但我若把主战写成别的什么而不写成一场混战，那便冒犯了史实的尊严——用史家的话说，那是一场“士兵的战斗”，一场马尔普拉凯（Malplaquet），一场阿尔武埃拉（Albuera）。

===Original===
Just after half-past three that afternoon the Commander-in-Chief was revealed in a very bad temper. He had intercepted Sir Archie’s car, and, since Léon was known to be fully occupied, had brought it in by the West Lodge, and hidden it behind a clump of laurels. There he had held a hoarse council of war. He had cast an appraising eye over Sime the butler, Carfrae the chauffeur, and McGuffog the gamekeeper, and his brows had lightened when he beheld Sir Archie with an armful of guns and two big cartridge-magazines. But they had darkened again at the first words of the leader of the reinforcements.

===Chinese===
那天下午三点半刚过，总司令大人便露出一副极坏的脾气。他半路截下了阿奇爵士的汽车，又因探知莱昂正忙得脱不开身，便让人把车从西门房开进来，藏在一丛月桂后头。就在那儿，他开了一场嗓音沙哑的军事会议。他把管家西姆、司机卡弗雷、猎场看守麦古福格上上下下估量了一遍，及至看见阿奇爵士抱来一摞枪和两大盒弹药，眉头一度舒展开来。可援兵统领开口的头一句话，又叫眉头重新拧紧。

===Original===
“Now for the Tower,” Sir Archie had observed cheerfully. “We should be a match for the three watchers, my lad, and it’s time that poor devil What’s-his-name was relieved.”

===Chinese===
“这就去攻塔，”阿奇爵士兴致勃勃地说。“对付那三个望风的，咱们绰绰有余，小伙子，再说，也该去换塔里那位可怜鬼——他叫什么来着——的班了。”

===Original===
“A bonny-like plan that would be,” said Dougal. “Man, ye would be walkin’ into the very trap they want. In an hour, or maybe two, the rest will turn up from the sea and they’d have ye tight by the neck. Na, na! It’s time we’re wantin’, and the longer they think we’re a’ in the auld Tower the better for us. What news o’ the polis?”

===Chinese===
“好一个美差！”杜加尔说。“伙计，你那是正往人家备好的圈套里钻。一个钟头，兴许两个钟头，海上那批人就到，到时候把你脖子掐得死死的。不，不！咱们眼下缺的就是工夫，他们越以为咱们全在老塔里，对咱们越有利。警察那边有什么信儿？”

===Original===
He listened to Sir Archie’s report with a gloomy face.

===Chinese===
他面色阴沉地听完了阿奇爵士的报告。

===Original===
“Not afore the darkenin’? They’ll be ower late﻿—the polis are aye ower late. It looks as if we had the job to do oursels. What’s *your* notion?”

===Chinese===
“擦黑以前都赶不上？那可就误了事——警察老是慢半拍。瞧这架势，这桩活计得咱们自个儿动手了。*你*有什么主意？”

===Original===
“God knows,” said the baronet whose eyes were on Saskia. “What’s yours?”

===Chinese===
“天晓得，”从男爵答道，眼睛却望着萨斯基亚。“你有什么主意？”

===Original===
The deference conciliated Dougal. “There’s just the one plan that’s worth a docken. There’s five o’ us here, and there’s plenty weapons. Besides there’s five Die-Hards somewhere about, and though they’ve never tried it afore they can be trusted to loose off a gun. My advice is to hide at the Garplefoot and stop the boats landin’. We’d have the tinklers on our flank, no doubt, but I’m not muckle feared o’ them. It wouldn’t be easy for the boats to get in wi’ this tearin’ wind and us firin’ volleys from the shore.”

===Chinese===
这份敬意把杜加尔安抚了下来。“拿得出手的方案只有一个。咱们这儿有五个人，枪有的是。外带还有五个死硬帮在附近什么地方，虽说他们从来没放过枪，这点总归靠得住。俺的主张是：埋伏在加普尔河下游，拦住小艇靠岸。两翼难免有补锅佬，这个不消说，可俺不太把他们放在心上。风刮得这么邪乎，咱们再从岸上齐射，小艇想靠岸可没那么容易。”

===Original===
Sir Archie stared at him with admiration. “You’re a hearty young fire-eater. But Great Scott! we can’t go pottin’ at strangers before we find out their business. This is a law-abidin’ country, and we’re not entitled to start shootin’ except in self-defence. You can wash that plan out, for it ain’t feasible.”

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士满眼钦佩地盯着他。“你是个血气十足的小主战派。可是老天爷！总不能人家来路还没弄清，就冲生人开火。这是个守法国家，除了正当防卫，咱们没资格先动手放枪。你这计划划掉吧，行不通的。”

===Original===
Dougal spat cynically. “For all that it’s the right strawtegy. Man, we might sink the lot, and then turn and settle wi’ Dobson, and all afore the first polisman showed his neb. It would be a grand performance. But I was feared ye wouldn’t be for it.﻿ ﻿… Well, there’s just the one other thing to do. We must get inside the Hoose and put it in a state of defence. Heritage has McCunn’s pistol, and he’ll keep them busy for a bit. When they’ve finished wi’ him and find the place is empty, they’ll try the Hoose and we’ll give them a warm reception. That should keep us goin’ till the polis arrive, unless they’re comin’ wi’ the blind carrier.”

===Chinese===
杜加尔冷冷啐了一口。“话虽这么说，那才是对路的战略。伙计，兴许能把那帮人一股脑儿送进海底，再回头收拾多布森，全办在头一名警察露头之前。那才叫一场漂亮戏。可俺就怕你们不干。……好吧，就剩另一条路了。咱们必须进府里去，把它布置成能守的样子。赫里蒂奇手里有麦坎的手枪，够他牵扯他们一阵子。等他们收拾完他，发现塔里空空如也，就会来打府里的主意，咱们就给他们个热热闹闹的迎接。这样怎么也能撑到警察来——除非他们这回是搭瞎眼邮车来的。”

===Original===
Sir Archie nodded. “But why put ourselves in their power at all? They’re at present barking up the wrong tree. Let them bark up another wrong ’un. Why shouldn’t the House remain empty? I take it we’re here to protect the Princess. Well, we’ll have done that if they go off empty-handed.”

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士点了点头。“可咱们何必自己送上门去？他们眼下是盯着错树的猎犬，就让他们再盯一棵错树好了。大宅为什么不能接着空着？照我看，咱们到这儿来是为了保护公主。只要他们两手空空地滚蛋，咱们就算大功告成。”

===Original===
Dougal looked up to the heavens. “I wish McCunn was here,” he sighed. “Ay, we’ve got to protect the Princess, and there’s just the one way to do it, and that’s to put an end to this crowd o’ blagyirds. If they gang empty-handed, they’ll come again another day, either here or somewhere else, and it won’t be long afore they get the lassie. But if we finish with them now she can sit down wi’ an easy mind. That’s why we’ve got to hang on to them till the polis comes. There’s no way out o’ this business but a battle.”

===Chinese===
杜加尔仰面望天。“麦坎在这儿就好了，”他叹道。“是了，咱们是要保护公主，可护住她的法子只有一条，就是把这帮匪棍做个了断。今天让他们空手走了，明天他们还会再来，不是在这儿，就是在别的什么地方，要不了多久就能把姑娘弄到手。可咱们要是眼下就把他们了结了，她就能安安稳稳坐得住了。所以咱们必须咬住他们不放，一直咬到警察来。这事没有别的出路，只有打一仗。”

===Original===
He found an ally. “Dougal is right,” said Saskia. “If I am to have peace, by some way or other the fangs of my enemies must be drawn forever.”

===Chinese===
他找到了一位盟友。“杜加尔说得对，”萨斯基亚道。“我若要得安宁，就得想个法子，把我仇家的毒牙永远拔了。”

===Original===
He swung round and addressed her formally. “Mem, I’m askin’ ye for the last time. Will ye keep out of this business? Will ye gang back and sit doun aside Mrs. Morran’s fire and have your tea and wait till we come for ye? Ye can do no good, and ye’re puttin’ yourself terrible in the enemy’s power. If we’re beat and ye’re no’ there, they get very little satisfaction, but if they get *you* they get what they’ve come seekin’. I tell ye straight﻿—ye’re an encumbrance.”

===Chinese===
他霍地转过身，郑重其事地对她说。“夫人，俺最后一回问您。您能不能别掺和这桩事？回莫兰太太的火炉边坐下，喝您的茶，等俺们来接您，成不成？您在这儿帮不上一点忙，反倒把您自个儿送到仇人手心里。俺们要是败了，您不在场，他们捞不着多少油水；可他们要是捞着了*您*，他们此行的目的就算达到了。俺跟您直说——您是个累赘。”

===Original===
She laughed mischievously. “I can shoot better than you,” she said.

===Chinese===
她淘气地笑了。“我的枪打得比你好。”她说。

===Original===
He ignored the taunt. “Will ye listen to sense and fall to the rear?”

===Chinese===
他不理会这句揶揄。“您肯不肯听劝，退到后头去？”

===Original===
“I will not,” she said.

===Chinese===
“我不肯。”她说。

===Original===
“Then gang your own gait. I’m ower wise to argy-bargy wi’ women. The Hoose be it!”

===Chinese===
“那您就走您的路吧。俺可不傻，不同娘们儿磨嘴皮子。进府就进府！”

===Original===
It was a journey which sorely tried Dougal’s temper. The only way in was by the verandah, but the door at the west end had been locked, and the ladder had disappeared. Now of his party three were lame, one lacked an arm, and one was a girl; besides, there were the guns and cartridges to transport. Moreover, at more than one point before the verandah was reached the route was commanded by a point on the ridge near the old Tower, and that had been Spidel’s position when Dougal made his last reconnaissance. It behoved to pass these points swiftly and unobtrusively, and his company was neither swift nor unobtrusive. McGuffog had a genius for tripping over obstacles, and Sir Archie was forever proffering his aid to Saskia, who was in a position to give rather than to receive, being far the most active of the party. Once Dougal had to take the gamekeeper’s head and force it down, a performance which would have led to an immediate assault but for Sir Archie’s presence. Nor did the latter escape. “Will ye stop heedin’ the lassie, and attend to your own job,” the Chieftain growled. “Ye’re makin’ as much noise as a road-roller.”

===Chinese===
这一路，把杜加尔的脾气磨得够呛。进宅唯一的路是走游廊，可游廊西端的门早叫人锁了，梯子也没了影。他那支队伍里，三个瘸子，一个缺胳膊，还有一个是姑娘；再说还有枪支弹药要搬运。更要命的是，没到游廊之前，有一段路正对着老塔近旁山脊上的一处高地，杜加尔上次侦察时，斯皮德尔就守在那儿。这些地段必须快步无声地通过，可他这一队人既谈不上快，也谈不上无声。麦古福格天生擅长叫绊子绊倒，阿奇爵士又一刻不停地向萨斯基亚献殷勤——其实全队数她最矫健，她照拂别人还差不多，哪里受人照拂。有一回杜加尔不得不亲手把猎场看守的脑袋按下去，若非阿奇爵士在场，这一按非引来一顿暴打不可。后者也没逃过训斥。“你能不能别盯着姑娘看，管管你自己的事，”首领咆哮道。“你闹出的动静，赶上压路机了。”

===Original===
Arrived at the foot of the verandah wall there remained the problem of the escalade. Dougal clambered up like a squirrel by the help of cracks in the stones, and he could be heard trying the handle of the door into the House. He was absent for about five minutes and then his head peeped over the edge accompanied by the hooks of an iron ladder. “From the boiler-house,” he informed them as they stood clear for the thing to drop. It proved to be little more than half the height of the wall.

===Chinese===
到了游廊墙根，剩下的问题就是攀登。杜加尔借石头缝儿手脚并用，松鼠似的蹿了上去，接着便听见他拨弄通往宅内的门把手的声音。他不见了约莫五分钟，然后一颗脑袋从墙沿探出来，伴随出现的是一架铁梯的挂钩。“锅炉房里弄来的，”他告诉众人，大家闪开身，看那玩意儿放下来。那梯子架起来，也不过墙高的一半多一点。

===Original===
Saskia ascended first, and had no difficulty in pulling herself over the parapet. Then came the guns and ammunition, and then the one-armed Sime, who turned out to be an athlete. But it was no easy matter getting up the last three. Sir Archie anathematised his frailties. “Nice old crock to go tiger-shootin’ with,” he told the Princess. “But set me to something where my confounded leg don’t get in the way, and I’m still pretty useful!” Dougal, mopping his brow with the rag he called his handkerchief, observed sourly that he objected to going scouting with a herd of elephants.

===Chinese===
萨斯基亚第一个上，翻过墙垛毫不费力。接着吊上枪支弹药，然后是独臂的西姆——此人居然是员运动健将。可最后那三位上来得就费了劲了。阿奇爵士把自己这副不中用的躯壳痛骂了一通。“带上这么个老病号去猎老虎，真是没得挑了，”他对公主说。“可只要派我干点那该死的腿不碍事的差事，我还是相当顶用的！”杜加尔用那块被他唤作手帕的破布抹着脑门，没好气地说，他反对领着一象群出去侦察。

===Original===
Once indoors his spirits rose. The party from the Mains had brought several electric torches and the one lamp was presently found and lit. “We can’t count on the polis,” Dougal announced, “and when the foreigners is finished wi’ the Tower they’ll come on here. If no’, we must make them. What is it the sodgers call it? Forcin’ a battle? Now see here! There’s the two roads into this place, the back door and the verandy, leavin’ out the front door which is chained and lockit. They’ll try those two roads first and we must get them well barricaded in time. But mind, if there’s a good few o’ them, it’ll be an easy job to batter in the front door or the windies, so we maun be ready for that.”

===Chinese===
一进宅子，他的精神就振作起来。田庄那拨人带来了好几支电筒，宅里仅有的一盏灯也很快找出来点上了。“警察是指望不上的，”杜加尔宣布，“外国佬收拾完塔里的人，就会朝这儿来。要是他们不来，咱们就逼他们来。大兵们管那叫什么来着？强求决战？都听着！进这宅子就两条路，后门和游廊——前门上了链条锁着，不算数。他们会先试这两条路，咱们得赶早把两处堵严实。可记着，他们人要是够多，砸开前门或者窗户都是容易事，所以咱们还得防着这一手。”

===Original===
He told off a fatigue party﻿—the Princess, Sir Archie and McGuffog﻿—to help in moving furniture to the several doors. Sime and Carfrae attended to the kitchen entrance, while he himself made a tour of the ground-floor windows. For half an hour the empty house was loud with strange sounds. McGuffog, who was a giant in strength, filled the passage at the verandah end with an assortment of furniture ranging from a grand piano to a vast mahogany sofa, while Saskia and Sir Archie pillaged the bedrooms and packed up the interstices with mattresses in lieu of sandbags. Dougal on his return saw fit to approve their work.

===Chinese===
他点起一支劳役队——公主、阿奇爵士和麦古福格——帮着往各处门口搬家具。西姆和卡弗雷照管厨房入口，他自己则把底层的窗户巡看了一遍。足足半个钟头，空宅里响彻了种种怪声。麦古福格力大如巨灵，把游廊那头的过道塞了个满满当当，家什从大钢琴到宽大的红木沙发一应俱全；萨斯基亚和阿奇爵士则洗劫了各个卧室，拿床垫充当沙袋，把缝隙填得严严实实。杜加尔回来转了一圈，觉得他们的活计还过得眼。

===Original===
“That’ll fickle the blagyirds. Down at the kitchen door we’ve got a mangle, five washtubs and the best part of a ton o’ coal. It’s the windies I’m anxious about, for they’re ower big to fill up. But I’ve gotten tubs o’ water below them and a lot o’ wire-nettin’ I fund in the cellar.”

===Chinese===
“这够那帮匪棍喝一壶的。厨房门那儿，俺们码了一台轧干机、五只洗衣桶，外加小半吨煤。俺放心不下的是窗户，窗洞太大，堵不严实。不过俺在窗下摆了水桶，还从地窖里翻出一大宗铁丝网，全用上了。”

===Original===
Sir Archie morosely wiped his brow. “I can’t say I ever hated a job more,” he told Saskia. “It seems pretty cool to march into somebody else’s house and make free with his furniture. I hope to goodness our friends from the sea do turn up, or we’ll look pretty foolish. Loudon will have a score against me he won’t forget.”

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士愁眉苦脸地抹了抹额头。“我这辈子没干过比这更叫我别扭的差事，”他对萨斯基亚说。“大摇大摆闯进别人家里，随便糟蹋人家的家具，未免太不客气了。我千恩万谢盼望海上那伙朋友真的露面，不然咱们可就洋相出足了。这笔账劳登会记在我头上，一辈子忘不掉。”

===Original===
“Ye’re no’ weakenin’?” asked Dougal fiercely.

===Chinese===
“你不是要泄气了吧？”杜加尔凶巴巴地问。

===Original===
“Not a bit. Only hopin’ somebody hasn’t made a mighty big mistake.”

===Chinese===
“一点儿也不。只是盼着别有谁闯了个天大的祸。”

===Original===
“Ye needn’t be feared for that. Now you listen to your instructions. We’re terrible few for such a big place, but we maun make up for shortness o’ numbers by extra mobility. The gemkeeper will keep the windy that looks on the verandy, and fell any man that gets through. You’ll hold the verandy door, and the ither lame man﻿—is’t Carfrae ye call him?﻿—will keep the back door. I’ve telled the one-armed man, who has some kind of a head on him, that he maun keep on the move, watchin’ to see if they try the front door or any o’ the other windies. If they do, he takes his station there. D’ye follow?”

===Chinese===
“这你不必担心。都竖起耳朵听令。守这么大的宅子，咱们人手少得可怜，只好拿勤跑动来补人数。看围子的守着朝游廊的那扇窗，谁钻进来就撂倒谁。你守游廊门，另一个瘸腿的——你们管他叫卡弗雷来着？——守后门。俺已经吩咐过那个独臂的——他脑袋瓜还使得——叫他不停地转悠，盯着他们会不会撬前门或旁的哪扇窗。真撬了，他就地驻守。听明白没有？”

===Original===
Sir Archie nodded gloomily. “What is my post?” Saskia asked.

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士蔫蔫地点了点头。“那我的岗位在哪儿？”萨斯基亚问。

===Original===
“I’ve appointed ye my Chief of Staff,” was the answer. “Ye see we’ve no reserves. If this door’s the dangerous bit, it maun be reinforced from elsewhere; and that’ll want savage thinkin’. Ye’ll have to be ay on the move, Mem, and keep me informed. If they break in at two bits, we’re beat, and there’ll be nothin’ for it but to retire to our last position. Ye ken the room ayont the hall where they keep the coats. That’s our last trench, and at the worst we fall back there and stick it out. It has a strong door and a wee windy, so they’ll no’ be able to get in on our rear. We should be able to put up a good defence there, unless they fire the place over our heads.﻿ ﻿… Now, we’d better give out the guns.”

===Chinese===
“俺任命您当俺的参谋长，”对方答道。“您知道，咱们没有预备队。哪扇门吃紧，就得从别处抽人去补，这可得狠狠动一番脑筋。您得一刻不停地走动，夫人，随时给俺报信儿。他们要是从两处同时破门，咱们就完了，只好退守最后的阵地。大厅那头存衣裳的那间屋子，您知道的。那就是咱们最后一道战壕，到了最坏的地步，就退到那儿死守。那屋门结实，只有一扇小窗，他们抄不了咱们的后路。在那儿咱们还能好好抵挡一阵，除非他们放火从咱们头顶烧过来。……好了，现在发枪。”

===Original===
“We don’t want any shootin’ if we can avoid it,” said Sir Archie, who found his distaste for Dougal growing, though he was under the spell of the one being there who knew precisely his own mind.

===Chinese===
“能不开枪，咱们就别开枪，”阿奇爵士说。他对杜加尔的嫌恶又添了一层，尽管他仍旧受着此间唯一一个自己心里透亮之人那股魔力的辖制。

===Original===
“Just what I was goin’ to say. My instructions is, reserve your fire, and don’t loose off till you have a man up against the end o’ your barrel.”

===Chinese===
“俺正要说这个。俺的命令是：留着火，枪口顶到人身上再放。”

===Original===
“Good Lord, we’ll get into a horrible row. The whole thing may be a mistake, and we’ll be had up for wholesale homicide. No man shall fire unless I give the word.”

===Chinese===
“老天爷，咱们要闯下滔天大祸了。这整桩事也许根本是个误会，到时咱们得背上一桩成批杀人的官司。我不发口令，谁也不许开枪。”

===Original===
The Commander-in-Chief looked at him darkly. Some bitter retort was on his tongue, but he restrained himself.

===Chinese===
总司令阴沉沉地看着他。一句刻毒的反唇相讥已经顶到舌尖，又被他咽了回去。

===Original===
“It appears,” he said, “that ye think I’m doin’ all this for fun. I’ll no ’argy wi’ ye. There can be just the one general in a battle, but I’ll give ye permission to say the word when to fire.﻿ ﻿… Macgreegor!” he muttered, a strange expletive only used in moments of deep emotion. “I’ll wager ye’ll be for sayin’ the word afore I’d say it mysel’.”

===Chinese===
“照这么说，”他道，“你是觉得俺干这一切是为了寻开心。俺不同你抬杠。一仗只能有一个统帅，可俺准你来说那声开火的口令。……麦格里高（Macgreegor）！”他咕哝出这句怪叹——只在心潮翻涌到极处的时刻才用。“俺跟你打赌，不等俺自己想喊，你就先喊了。”

===Original===
He turned to the Princess. “I hand over to you, till I am back, for I maun be off and see to the Die-Hards. I wish I could bring them in here, but I daren’t lose my communications. I’ll likely get in by the boiler-house skylight when I come back, but it might be as well to keep a road open here unless ye’re actually attacked.”

===Chinese===
他转向公主。“俺回来之前，这摊子交给您了，俺得去看看死硬帮那头。俺想把他们带进宅里来，可俺不敢断了联络。回来的时候，俺多半从锅炉房的天窗进来，不过这儿的路最好也留着，别堵死，除非他们真打上门来。”

===Original===
Dougal clambered over the mattresses and the grand piano; a flicker of waning daylight appeared for a second as he squeezed through the door, and Sir Archie was left staring at the wrathful countenance of McGuffog. He laughed ruefully.

===Chinese===
杜加尔翻过床垫和大钢琴走了；他挤出门的一瞬，一线残昼的天光闪了一闪，屋里剩下阿奇爵士，对着麦古福格那张怒气冲冲的脸发怔。他苦笑了一声。

===Original===
“I’ve been in about forty battles, and here’s that little devil rather worried about my pluck, and talkin’ to me like a corps commander to a newly joined second-lieutenant. All the same he’s a remarkable child, and we’d better behave as if we were in for a real shindy. What do you think, Princess?”

===Chinese===
“我打过四十来仗了，如今倒好，这个小魔头居然操心起我的胆量来，跟我讲话的派头，活像军长训一个刚报到的少尉。不过话说回来，这孩子真是个人物，咱们最好还是拿出真要打一场恶仗的架势来对付。你说呢，公主？”

===Original===
“I think we are in for what you call a shindy. I am in command, remember. I order you to serve out the guns.”

===Chinese===
“我想咱们确实要打一场你所说的恶仗了。现在由我指挥，记住。我命令你，发枪。”

===Original===
This was done, a shotgun and a hundred cartridges to each, while McGuffog, who was a marksman, was also given a sporting Mannlicher, and two other rifles, a .303 and a small-bore Holland, were kept in reserve in the hall. Sir Archie, free from Dougal’s compelling presence, gave the gamekeeper peremptory orders not to shoot till he was bidden, and Carfrae at the kitchen door was warned to the same effect. The shuttered house, where the only light apart from the garden-room was the feeble spark of the electric torches, had the most disastrous effect upon his spirits. The gale which roared in the chimney and eddied among the rafters of the hall seemed an infernal commotion in a tomb.

===Chinese===
枪发下去了，每人一支霰弹枪、一百发子弹；神枪手麦古福格额外得了一支猎用曼利夏（Mannlicher），另有两支步枪——一支.303，一支小口径霍兰德（Holland）——留在厅里作预备。没了杜加尔那股咄咄逼人的气场，阿奇爵士用不容分说的口吻吩咐猎场看守，不叫他开枪就不得开枪，厨房门口的卡弗雷也得到了同样郑重的警告。这座百叶紧闭的宅子，除了花园厅（garden-room）那盏灯，照明的只有电筒里荧荧的微光，对他的心绪产生了灾难性的影响。狂风在烟囱里轰鸣，在大厅的椽木间打旋，恍如坟墓里一场地狱般的骚动。

===Original===
“Let’s go upstairs,” he told Saskia; “there must be a view from the upper windows.”

===Chinese===
“咱们上楼去吧，”他对萨斯基亚说。“楼上的窗户总该望得见些动静。”

===Original===
“You can see the top of the old Tower, and part of the sea,” she said. “I know it well, for it was my only amusement to look at it. On clear days, too, one could see high mountains far in the west.” His depression seemed to have affected her, for she spoke listlessly, unlike the vivid creature who had led the way in.

===Chinese===
“望得见老塔的塔顶，还有一角海，”她说。“我熟得很，望这个是我唯一的消遣。天晴的日子，还能远远望见西边的高山。”他的低沉似乎传染了她，她说话无精打采，全不像方才领着大伙儿进宅时那个神采飞扬的人。

===Original===
In a gaunt west-looking bedroom, the one in which Heritage and Dickson had camped the night before, they opened a fold of the shutters and looked out into a world of grey wrack and driving rain. The Tower roof showed mistily beyond the ridge of down, but its environs were not in their prospect. The lower regions of the House had been gloomy enough, but this bleak place with its drab outlook struck a chill to Sir Archie’s soul. He dolefully lit a cigarette.

===Chinese===
在一间阴沉沉的朝西卧室里——正是头天夜里赫里蒂奇和迪克森宿营的那间——他们掀开一角百叶，朝外望去，外面是一个灰絮翻卷、骤雨横飞的世界。荒丘的脊线之外，塔顶隐约可辨，塔的四周却不在视野之内。宅子的底下几层已够阴郁，这间凄冷的屋子配上窗外灰蒙蒙的一片，更把一股寒气直注进阿奇爵士心里。他怏怏地点起一支烟。

===Original===
“This is a pretty rotten show for you,” he told her. “It strikes me as a rather unpleasant brand of nightmare.”

===Chinese===
“让你摊上这么一出戏，真是糟透了，”他对她说。“依我看，这是噩梦里面顶叫人难受的一个牌子。”

===Original===
“I have been living with nightmares for three years,” she said wearily.

===Chinese===
“我同噩梦一起过了三年了。”她倦倦地说。

===Original===
He cast his eyes round the room. “I think the Kennedys were mad to build this confounded barrack. I’ve always disliked it, and old Quentin hadn’t any use for it either. Cold, cheerless, raw monstrosity! It hasn’t been a very giddy place for you, Princess.”

===Chinese===
他环视了一下屋子。“我看肯尼迪家的人是疯了，才会盖这么座该死的大兵营。我一向就不喜欢它，老昆廷也从没拿它当回事。又冷、又阴森、丑得刺眼的怪物！这地方对你来说，实在没什么可开心的，公主。”

===Original===
“It has been my prison, when I hoped it would be a sanctuary. But it may yet be my salvation.”

===Chinese===
“我本指望它是我的避难所，它却做了我的牢狱。不过，它或许还会成为我的救星。”

===Original===
“I’m sure I hope so. I say, you must be jolly hungry. I don’t suppose there’s any chance of tea for you.”

===Chinese===
“我真心盼着如此。我说，你一定饿坏了吧。我看，想给你弄杯茶，是指望不上了。”

===Original===
She shook her head. She was looking fixedly at the Tower, as if she expected something to appear there, and he followed her eyes.

===Chinese===
她摇了摇头。她正目不转睛地望着那座塔，仿佛料定那里会出现什么，他顺着她的目光望去。

===Original===
“Rum old shell, that. Quentin used to keep all kinds of live stock there, and when we were boys it was our castle where we played at bein’ robber chiefs. It’ll be dashed queer if the real thing should turn up this time. I suppose McCunn’s Poet is roostin’ there all by his lone. Can’t say I envy him his job.”

===Chinese===
“那老壳子真是个古怪地方。老昆廷从前在里头养过各色活物，咱们小时候，它是咱们的城堡，咱们在里头扮强盗大王玩。这回要是把真强盗盼来了，那才叫邪门呢。我琢磨，麦坎的那位诗人这会儿正孤零零一个在里头蹲着呢。说实在的，我可不眼馋他那份差事。”

===Original===
Suddenly she caught his arm. “I see a man,” she whispered. “There! He is behind those far bushes. There is his head again!”

===Chinese===
突然，她一把抓住他的胳膊。“我看见一个人，”她低声道。“在那儿！他在远处那些树丛后面。他的头又露出来了！”

===Original===
It was clearly a man, but he presently disappeared, for he had come round by the south end of the House, past the stables, and had now gone over the ridge.

===Chinese===
那分明是个人，不过随即就不见了：他是从大宅南头绕过来的，过了马厩，这会儿已经翻过山脊去了。

===Original===
“The cut of his jib is uncommonly like Loudon, the factor. I thought McCunn had stretched him on a bed of pain. Lord, if this thing should turn out a farce, I simply can’t face Loudon.﻿ ﻿… I say, Princess, you don’t suppose by any chance that McCunn’s a little bit wrong in the head?”

===Chinese===
“那副长相，像极了代管人劳登。我还当麦坎已经把他安顿到病床上了呢。老天，这桩事要真成了一场闹剧，我可简直没脸再见劳登了。……我说，公主，你该不会觉得，麦坎这人脑子有点毛病吧？”

===Original===
She turned her candid eyes on him. “You are in a very doubting mood.”

===Chinese===
她把那双坦率的眼睛转向他。“你今天满肚子都是疑心。”

===Original===
“My feet are cold and I don’t mind admittin’ it. Hanged if I know what it is, but I don’t feel this show a bit real. If it isn’t, we’re in a fair way to make howlin’ idiots of ourselves, and get pretty well embroiled with the law. It’s all right for the red-haired boy, for he can take everything seriously, even play. I could do the same thing myself when I was a kid. I don’t mind runnin’ some kinds of risk﻿—I’ve had a few in my time﻿—but this is so infernally outlandish and I﻿—I don’t quite believe in it. That is to say, I believe in it right enough when I look at you or listen to McCunn, but as soon as my eyes are off you I begin to doubt again. I’m gettin’ old and I’ve a stake in the country, and I daresay I’m gettin’ a bit of a prig﻿—anyway I don’t want to make a jackass of myself. Besides, there’s this foul weather and this beastly house to ice my feet.”

===Chinese===
“我心里发虚，脚底发凉，这我不讳言。鬼知道是怎么回事，我总觉得这出戏不真。要是不真，咱们正顺顺当当地把自己变成两个嚎啕大叫的傻瓜，还得同法律大大地纠缠一番。红头发那小子倒不要紧，他什么都能当真，连玩儿都当真。我小时候也行。有些风险我不在乎冒——我这辈子也冒过几回——可这事实在邪门得没边儿，我——我信不真。这么说吧，看着你，或是听麦坎说话，我信得十足；可眼睛一离开你，我就又疑心起来。我上了年纪，在这一郡有身家产业，没准儿也变得有点道学了——反正我不想把自己变成一头蠢驴。再说，还有这恶劣的天气、这可憎的宅子，给我的脚底板降温。”

===Original===
He broke off with an exclamation, for on the grey cloud-bounded stage in which the roof of the Tower was the central feature, actors had appeared. Dim hurrying shapes showed through the mist, dipping over the ridge, as if coming from the Garplefoot.

===Chinese===
他一声惊呼，话断了——在那片以塔顶为中央布景的灰云四合的舞台上，演员登场了。雾气里显现出影影绰绰疾行的身影，正翻下山脊，像是从加普尔河下游来的。

===Original===
She seized his arm and he saw that her listlessness was gone. Her eyes were shining.

===Chinese===
她一把抓住他的胳膊，他看出她的委顿一扫而空。她两眼放光。

===Original===
“It is they,” she cried. “The nightmare is real at last. Do you doubt now?”

===Chinese===
“是他们，”她喊道。“噩梦终于成真了。现在你还疑心吗？”

===Original===
He could only stare, for these shapes arriving and vanishing like wisps of fog still seemed to him phantasmal. The girl held his arm tightly clutched, and craned towards the window space. He tried to open the frame, and succeeded in smashing the glass. A swirl of wind drove inwards and blew a loose lock of Saskia’s hair across his brow.

===Chinese===
他只能干瞪眼——这些身影像雾缕般忽而聚来、忽而散去，在他眼里仍似幻影。姑娘紧紧攥住他的胳膊，探身朝窗口凑去。他想把窗框推开，结果把玻璃捣了个粉碎。一股旋风灌进屋来，把萨斯基亚一绺散开的头发吹到了他额前。

===Original===
“I wish Dougal were back,” he muttered, and then came the crack of a shot.

===Chinese===
“杜加尔回来就好了。”他咕哝道。话音未落，一声枪响炸开。

===Original===
The pressure on his arm slackened, and a pale face was turned to him. “He is alone﻿—Mr. Heritage. He has no chance. They will kill him like a dog.”

===Chinese===
胳膊上那只手的力道松了，一张苍白的脸转向他。“他只一个人——赫里蒂奇先生。他一点指望也没有。他们会像宰狗一样宰了他。”

===Original===
“They’ll never get in,” he assured her. “Dougal said the place could hold out for hours.”

===Chinese===
“他们进不去的，”他安慰她道。“杜加尔说了，那地方撑得住好几个钟头。”

===Original===
Another shot followed and presently a third. She twined her hands and her eyes were wild.

===Chinese===
接着又是一枪，不多时第三枪。她绞着双手，眼里一片狂乱。

===Original===
“We can’t leave him to be killed,” she gasped.

===Chinese===
“咱们不能撂下他让人打死。”她喘着气说。

===Original===
“It’s the only game. We’re playin’ for time, remember. Besides he won’t be killed. Great Scott!”

===Chinese===
“这是唯一的打法。别忘了，咱们赌的是时间。再说他死不了。天哪！”

===Original===
As he spoke, a sudden explosion cleft the drone of the wind and a patch of gloom flashed into yellow light.

===Chinese===
话音未落，一声突如其来的爆炸劈开了风声的嗡鸣，一片幽暗里猛地闪出黄光。

===Original===
“Bomb!” he cried. “Lord, I might have thought of that.”

===Chinese===
“炸弹！”他喊道。“老天，我怎么就没先想到这一手。”

===Original===
The girl had sprung back from the window. “I cannot bear it. I will not see him murdered in sight of his friends. I am going to show myself, and when they see me they will leave him.﻿ ﻿… No, you must stay here. Presently they will be round this house. Don’t be afraid for me﻿—I am very quick of foot.”

===Chinese===
姑娘已从窗前跳开。“我受不了了。我不能眼看着他在朋友们的眼面前被害。我要现身出去，他们一看见我，就会丢下他。……不，你必须留在这儿。他们马上就要围这座宅子了。别为我担心——我腿脚快得很。”

===Original===
“For God’s sake, don’t! Here, Princess, stop,” and he clutched at her skirt. “Look here, I’ll go.”

===Chinese===
“千万使不得！喂，公主，站住，”他一把去抓她的裙子。“你听我说，我去。”

===Original===
“You can’t. You have been wounded. I am in command, you know. Keep the door open till I come back.”

===Chinese===
“你去不了。你带着伤。别忘了，现在是我指挥。把门留着别关，等我回来。”

===Original===
He hobbled after her, but she easily eluded him. She was smiling now, and blew a kiss to him. “La, la, la,” she trilled, as she ran down the stairs. He heard her voice below, admonishing McGuffog. Then he pulled himself together and went back to the window. He had brought the little Holland with him, and he poked its barrel through the hole in the glass.

===Chinese===
他一瘸一拐地追下去，却被她轻巧地甩脱了。这会儿她笑了，还给他一个飞吻。“啦，啦，啦，”她哼着花腔跑下楼梯。他听见她在楼下叮嘱麦古福格的声音。随后他定了定神，回到窗前。他随身带了那支小霍兰德，便把枪管从玻璃的破洞里探了出去。

===Original===
“Curse my game leg,” he said, almost cheerfully, for the situation was now becoming one with which he could cope. “I ought to be able to hold up the pursuit a bit. My aunt! What a girl!”

===Chinese===
“该死的瘸腿。”他说这话时几乎带着欢快，因为眼下的局面总算变成他应付得了的那种了。“我总还能替她拖一拖追兵。嗬！好一个姑娘！”

===Original===
With the rifle cuddled to his shoulder he watched a slim figure come into sight on the lawn, running towards the ridge. He reflected that she must have dropped from the high verandah wall. That reminded him that something must be done to make the wall climbable for her return, so he went down to McGuffog, and the two squeezed through the barricaded door to the verandah. The boiler-house ladder was still in position, but it did not reach half the height, so McGuffog was adjured to stand by to help, and in the meantime to wait on duty by the wall. Then he hurried upstairs to his watchtower.

===Chinese===
他把步枪贴肩搂定，望着草坪上一个纤细的身影奔出来，直奔山脊而去。他寻思，她必定是从高高的游廊墙上跳下去的。这倒提醒了他：得想法子让那堵墙好爬，好接她回来。于是他下楼寻着麦古福格，两人从堵严的门里挤上游廊。锅炉房那架梯子还架在原处，却够不到墙高的一半，他便叮嘱麦古福格守在梯旁接应，暂且就在墙边值哨。然后他匆匆赶回楼上了望哨去。

===Original===
The girl was in sight, almost on the crest of the high ground. There she stood for a moment, one hand clutching at her errant hair, the other shielding her eyes from the sting of the rain. He heard her cry, as Heritage had heard her, but since the wind was blowing towards him the sound came louder and fuller. Again she cried, and then stood motionless with her hands above her head. It was only for an instant, for the next he saw she had turned and was racing down the slope, jumping the little scrogs of hazel like a deer. On the ridge appeared faces, and then over it swept a mob of men.

===Chinese===
那姑娘还在视野里，差不多已到高地的脊顶。她在那儿伫立了片刻，一只手抓着被风搅乱的头发，另一只手遮在眼上挡雨。他听见她的呼喊——赫里蒂奇也曾听见——只因风朝他这边吹，声音传得更响、更饱满。她再喊了一声，随即双手高举过头，一动不动地立定。这只是刹那间的事，再一定睛，她已转身朝坡下狂奔，小鹿般跃过一丛丛小小的榛树苗。山脊上现出一张张面孔，跟着，一大群人漫过脊顶席卷而下。

===Original===
She had a start of some fifty yards, and laboured to increase it, having doubtless the verandah wall in mind. Sir Archie, sick with anxiety, nevertheless spared time to admire her prowess. “Gad! she’s a miler,” he ejaculated. “She’ll do it. I’m hanged if she don’t do it.”

===Chinese===
她先跑了约莫五十码，还拼命想拉开距离，心里必定惦记着那道游廊墙。阿奇爵士急得发慌，却仍分神赞叹她的身手。“天哪！她是员一英里跑将。”他脱口喊道。“她准能到。到不了我把脑袋输给你。”

===Original===
Against men in seaman’s boots and heavy clothing she had a clear advantage. But two shook themselves loose from the pack and began to gain on her. At the main shrubbery they were not thirty yards behind, and in her passage through it her skirts must have delayed her, for when she emerged the pursuit had halved the distance. He got the sights of the rifle on the first man, but the lawns sloped up towards the house, and to his consternation he found that the girl was in the line of fire. Madly he ran to the other window of the room, tore back the shutters, shivered the glass, and flung his rifle to his shoulder. The fellow was within three yards of her, but thank God! he had now a clear field. He fired low and just ahead of him, and had the satisfaction to see him drop like a rabbit, shot in the leg. His companion stumbled over him, and for a moment the girl was safe.

===Chinese===
对方穿着水手靴、裹着厚衣裳，同他们相比她占了十足的上风。可有两个人从群里挣脱出来，渐渐追近了她。到主灌木林那头，他们离她已不足三十码；她穿林而过时，裙子必是拖累了脚程，因为她钻出树丛时，距离又缩短了一半。他把步枪准星套住了头一个人，可草坪朝宅子这边一路高上去，他惊觉那姑娘正处在弹道上。他疯了似的冲向屋里另一扇窗，拽开百叶，捣碎玻璃，把枪甩上肩。那家伙离她只有三码了，可谢天谢地！这下视野清净了。他朝那人前方偏下打了一枪，心满意足地看他像只兔子似的栽倒，腿上着了一弹。他的同伙在他身上绊了个趔趄，一时间，姑娘安全了。

===Original===
But her speed was failing. She passed out of sight on the verandah side of the house, and the rest of the pack had gained ominously over the easier ground of the lawn. He thought for a moment of trying to stop them by his fire, but realised that if every shot told there would still be enough of them left to make sure of her capture. The only chance was at the verandah, and he went downstairs at a pace undreamed of since the days when he had two whole legs.

===Chinese===
可她的速度垮下来了。她在宅子游廊一侧没了踪影，余下的追兵在平顺的草坪上追近了一大截，势头凶险。他一时想用自己的火力拦阻，随即省悟：纵然枪枪命中，剩下的人手也尽够稳稳把她拿住。唯一的胜算在游廊，于是他往楼下赶，那步子，是他两条腿还齐全的年月里也不曾梦想过的。

===Original===
McGuffog, Mannlicher in hand, was poking his neck over the wall. The pursuit had turned the corner and were about twenty yards off; the girl was at the foot of the ladder, breathless, drooping with fatigue. She tried to climb, limply and feebly, and very slowly, as if she were too giddy to see clear. Above were two cripples, and at her back the van of the now triumphant pack.

===Chinese===
麦古福格提着曼利夏，正探着脖子朝墙外张望。追兵已拐过屋角，离着约莫二十码；姑娘到了梯子脚下，气息奄奄，累得直不起身。她试着往上爬，软塌乏力，慢得可怜，仿佛晕得看不清眼前。上头是两个残废，她背后，是眼看得手、得意洋洋的追兵前锋。

===Original===
Sir Archie, game leg or no, was on the parapet preparing to drop down and hold off the pursuit were it only for seconds. But at that moment he was aware that the situation had changed.

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士顾不得那条瘸腿了，跨上墙垛，只等跳下去把追兵挡上一挡，哪怕只挡几秒钟。可就在这时，他察觉局面变了。

===Original===
At the foot of the ladder a tall man seemed to have sprung out of the ground. He caught the girl in his arms, climbed the ladder, and McGuffog’s great hands reached down and seized her and swung her into safety. Up the wall, by means of cracks and tufts, was shinning a small boy.

===Chinese===
梯子脚下，一个大个子仿佛从地里凭空冒了出来。他把姑娘揽进臂弯，攀上梯子，麦古福格的两只大手探下来，一把将她拽过墙头，送到安全处。墙上，还有个小家伙正抠着石缝草根往上爬。

===Original===
The stranger coolly faced the pursuers and at the sight of him they checked, those behind stumbling against those in front. He was speaking to them in a foreign tongue, and to Sir Archie’s ear the words were like the crack of a lash. The hesitation was only for a moment, for a voice among them cried out, and the whole pack gave tongue shrilly and surged on again. But that instant of check had given the stranger his chance. He was up the ladder, and, gripping the parapet, found rest for his feet in a fissure. Then he bent down, drew up the ladder, handed it to McGuffog and with a mighty heave pulled himself over the top.

===Chinese===
那陌生人从容地转过身来面对追兵；一看见他，追兵收住了脚，后面的撞在前面的身上。他冲他们说着一种外国话，在阿奇爵士耳中，那些字句像鞭梢炸响。迟疑只持续了一瞬——人群里有人喊了一声，整群人就尖声鼓噪着重新涌上来。但那一瞬的迟疑已给了陌生人可乘之机。他蹬上梯子，攀住墙垛，一只脚在石缝里找到依托。随后他俯下身，把梯子拽上来递给麦古福格，自己猛一发力，翻过了墙头。

===Original===
He seemed to hope to defend the verandah, but the door at the west end was being assailed by a contingent of the enemy, and he saw that its thin woodwork was yielding.

===Chinese===
他似乎还指望守住游廊，可西端的门正遭到一支敌队的攻打，他眼见那层薄薄的门板就要让出来了。

===Original===
“Into the House,” he cried, as he picked up the ladder and tossed it over the wall on the pack surging below. He was only just in time, for the west door yielded. In two steps he had followed McGuffog through the chink into the passage, and the concussion of the grand piano pushed hard against the verandah door from within coincided with the first battering on the said door from without.

===Chinese===
“进宅里去。”他喊道，一面拎起梯子，朝墙下涌动的人群掼了下去。他来得恰是时候，因为西门的门板紧接着就破了。他两步跨过缝隙，跟着麦古福格进了过道；与此同时，里头死死抵住游廊门的大钢琴被撞得一声闷响，正赶上外头撞上门的第一记重击。

===Original===
In the garden-room the feeble lamp showed a strange grouping. Saskia had sunk into a chair to get her breath, and seemed too dazed to be aware of her surroundings. Dougal was manfully striving to appear at his ease, but his lip was quivering.

===Chinese===
花园厅里，那盏半明半暗的灯照出一幅奇特的群像。萨斯基亚跌坐进一把椅子里喘息，神情恍惚，仿佛全然不觉周遭的一切。杜加尔竭力装出气定神闲的样子，嘴唇却在发颤。

===Original===
“A near thing that time,” he observed. “It was the blame of that man’s auld motor-bicycle.”

===Chinese===
“方才好险，”他说。“都怪那人那辆老掉牙的摩托。”

===Original===
The stranger cast sharp eyes around the place and company.

===Chinese===
那陌生人一双锐利的眼睛，把屋子和大伙儿挨个扫了一遍。

===Original===
“An awkward corner, gentlemen,” he said. “How many are there of you? Four men and a boy? And you have placed guards at all the entrances?”

===Chinese===
“诸位，局面相当棘手，”他说。“你们有多少人？四条汉子和一个孩子？各处入口都布了岗？”

===Original===
“They have bombs,” Sir Archie reminded him.

===Chinese===
“他们有炸弹。”阿奇爵士提醒他道。

===Original===
“No doubt. But I do not think they will use them here﻿—or their guns, unless there is no other way. Their purpose is kidnapping, and they hope to do it secretly and slip off without leaving a trace. If they slaughter us, as they easily can, the cry will be out against them, and their vessel will be unpleasantly hunted. Half their purpose is already spoiled, for it is no longer secret.﻿ ﻿… They may break us by sheer weight, and I fancy the first shooting will be done by us. It’s the windows I’m afraid of.”

===Chinese===
“毫无疑问。但我想他们不会在此地动用炸弹——也不会动枪，除非别无出路。他们的目的是绑人，指望悄悄办成，不留一丝痕迹便扬长而去。倘若他们屠戮我们——这他们轻易办得到——声讨的怒吼便会追住他们不放，他们的船也会落得被穷追猛缉的下场。他们的图谋已经毁了一半，因为它不再是秘密了。……他们也许会凭人多的分量硬压垮我们，而且我料想，头一枪会由我们来放。我怕的是窗户。”

===Original===
Some tone in his quiet voice reached the girl in the wicker chair. She looked up wildly, saw him and with a cry of “Alesha” ran to his arms. There she hung, while his hand fondled her hair, like a mother with a scared child. Sir Archie, watching the whole thing in some stupefaction, thought he had never in his days seen more nobly matched human creatures.

===Chinese===
他那平静嗓音里的某种音调，触到了柳条椅里的姑娘。她狂乱地抬头一望，看见了他，喊一声“阿廖沙（Alesha）”，扑进他怀里。她偎在那里，他一手抚弄她的头发，像母亲哄一个受惊的孩子。阿奇爵士有些发懵地看完了这一幕，心想他这辈子，从没见过比这更天造地设的一双人。

===Original===
“It is my friend,” she cried triumphantly, “the friend whom I appointed to meet me here. Oh, I did well to trust him. Now we need not fear anything.”

===Chinese===
“他是我的朋友，”她欢声叫道，得意洋洋，“我约好在此地会面的那位朋友。啊，我信他没有信错。这下我们什么都不用怕了。”

===Original===
As if in ironical answer came a great crashing at the verandah door, and the twanging of chords cruelly mishandled. The grand piano was suffering internally from the assaults of the boiler-house ladder.

===Chinese===
仿佛为了应景作答似的，游廊门外传来一声天崩地裂的巨响，外加琴弦惨遭毒手的铮鸣。那架大钢琴隔着门板，正承受着锅炉房梯子的连番捶打。

===Original===
“Wull I gie them a shot?” was McGuffog’s hoarse inquiry.

===Chinese===
“要不要俺赏他们一枪？”麦古福格哑着嗓子请示。

===Original===
“Action stations,” Alexis ordered, for the command seemed to have shifted to him from Dougal. “The windows are the danger. The boy will patrol the ground floor, and give us warning, and I and this man,” pointing to Sime, “will be ready at the threatened point. And for God’s sake no shooting, unless I give the word. If we take them on at that game we haven’t a chance.”

===Chinese===
“各就战位。”亚历克西斯下令道，指挥权似乎已从杜加尔手里移到了他身上。“危险在窗户。孩子巡查底层，负责报警，我同这个人，”他指了指西姆，“随时赶赴吃紧的地点。还有，看在上帝分上，谁也不许开枪，除非我下令。真同他们斗起枪来，我们毫无胜算。”

===Original===
He said something to Saskia in Russian and she smiled assent and went to Sir Archie’s side. “You and I must keep this door,” she said.

===Chinese===
他用俄语对萨斯基亚说了句什么，她微笑着点头应允，走到阿奇爵士身旁。“这扇门由你我同守。”她说。

===Original===
Sir Archie was never very clear afterwards about the events of the next hour. The Princess was in the maddest spirits, as if the burden of three years had slipped from her and she was back in her first girlhood. She sang as she carried more lumber to the pile﻿—perhaps the song which had once entranced Heritage, but Sir Archie had no ear for music. She mocked at the furious blows which rained at the other end, for the door had gone now, and in the windy gap could be seen a blur of dark faces. Oddly enough, he found his own spirits mounting to meet hers. It was real business at last, the qualms of the civilian had been forgotten, and there was rising in him that joy in a scrap which had once made him one of the most daring airmen on the Western Front. The only thing that worried him now was the coyness about shooting. What on earth were his rifles and shotguns for unless to be used? He had seen the enemy from the verandah wall, and a more ruffianly crew he had never dreamed of. They meant the uttermost business, and against such it was surely the duty of good citizens to wage wholehearted war.

===Chinese===
事后，阿奇爵士对这一个钟头里发生的事，始终没能在心里理出个清楚的条理。公主兴致高得近乎发狂，仿佛三年的重担从肩上一滑而落，她又回到了无忧无虑的少女时代。她搬木料垒路障时还唱着歌——唱的兴许就是那支曾令赫里蒂奇神魂颠倒的歌，只是阿奇爵士没有欣赏音乐的耳朵。那头游廊门已经破了，狂怒的捶击雨点般落下，进风的豁口里望得见一团团模糊的黑脸，她却偏拿这些打趣。说来也怪，他发觉自己的兴致也一路高涨着迎了上去。这终于不是儿戏了，平民的种种疑怯被忘了个干净，他胸中正涌起那股干仗的欢畅——正是这股欢畅，当年曾使他跻身西线最亡命的飞行员之列。如今唯一叫他烦心的，是不许开枪这条规矩。他的步枪和霰弹枪，不为放枪还图个什么？他从游廊墙上望见过敌人，那等凶神恶煞的一伙，他做梦也不曾梦见过。他们是要把买卖干到底的，对这样的货色，好公民理当全力以赴，同他们痛痛快快打上一仗。

===Original===
The Princess was humming to herself a nursery rhyme. “The King of Spain’s daughter,” she crooned, “came to visit me, and all for the sake﻿—Oh, that poor piano!” In her clear voice she cried something in Russian, and the wind carried a laugh from the verandah. At the sound of it she stopped. “I had forgotten,” she said. “Paul is there. I had forgotten.” After that she was very quiet, but she redoubled her labours at the barricade.

===Chinese===
公主独自哼起一支童谣。“西班牙国王的女儿，”她低声唱道，“专程来把我探望，全都是为了那棵——哦，可怜的钢琴！”她忽然用清亮的嗓子喊了句俄语，风从游廊那头捎回一声大笑。听见那声笑，她停住了。“我忘了，”她说。“保罗在那边呢。我忘了。”此后她便十分安静，只是垒路障的干劲加倍了。

===Original===
To the man it seemed that the pressure from without was slackening. He called to McGuffog to ask about the garden-room window, and the reply was reassuring. The gamekeeper was gloomily contemplating Dougal’s tubs of water and wire-netting, as he might have contemplated a vermin trap.

===Chinese===
在他看来，外面的攻势似乎松了劲。他扬声问麦古福格花园厅那扇窗的情况，回答叫人宽心。猎场看守正愁眉苦脸地打量着杜加尔的水桶和铁丝网，那神情，像在打量一副捕兽夹。

===Original===
Sir Archie was growing acutely anxious﻿—the anxiety of the defender of a straggling fortress which is vulnerable at a dozen points. It seemed to him that strange noises were coming from the rooms beyond the hall. Did the back door lie that way? And was not there a smell of smoke in the air? If they tried fire in such a gale the place would burn like matchwood.

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士的焦灼一寸寸锋利起来——那是一座摊得太开、十几个口子都挨得着打的堡垒守将的焦灼。他仿佛听得大厅那头的屋子里透出怪声。后门是不是在那个方向？空气里是不是还飘着一丝烟味？这样的狂风里他们若用火攻，整座宅子会像火柴匣一样烧个精光。

===Original===
He left his post and in the hall found Dougal.

===Chinese===
他离了岗位，在大厅里找到杜加尔。

===Original===
“All quiet,” the Chieftain reported. “Far ower quiet. I don’t like it. The enemy’s no’ puttin’ out his strength yet. The Russian says a’ the west windies are terrible dangerous. Him and the chauffeur’s doin’ their best, but ye can’t block thae muckle glass panes.”

===Chinese===
“一切平静，”首领报告道。“平静得过了头。俺不喜欢这样。敌人还没拿出真本事。那俄国人说，朝西的窗户全都是天大的危险。他和司机正尽力堵，可那么大的玻璃板子，你没法子堵死。”

===Original===
He returned to the Princess, and found that the attack had indeed languished on that particular barricade. The withers of the grand piano were left unwrung, and only a faint scuffling informed him that the verandah was not empty. “They’re gathering for an attack elsewhere,” he told himself. But what if that attack were a feint? He and McGuffog must stick to their post, for in his belief the verandah door and the garden-room window were the easiest places where an entry in mass could be forced.

===Chinese===
他回到公主身边，发现那道路障前的攻势当真懈了。大钢琴这回总算免了受难，只有一阵轻微的窸窣声告诉他，游廊上并不是空的。“他们在别处集结，要发动进攻。”他暗自判断。可万一那一处只是佯攻呢？他和麦古福格必须死守岗位，因为依他看，游廊门和花园厅的窗，是敌人最便于大举突入的两处。

===Original===
Suddenly Dougal’s whistle blew, and with it came a most almighty crash somewhere towards the west side. With a shout of “Hold tight, McGuffog,” Sir Archie bolted into the hall, and, led by the sound, reached what had once been the ladies’ bedroom. A strange sight met his eyes, for the whole framework of one window seemed to have been thrust inward, and in the gap Alexis was swinging a fender. Three of the enemy were in the room﻿—one senseless on the floor, one in the grip of Sime, whose single hand was tightly clenched on his throat, and one engaged with Dougal in a corner. The Die-Hard leader was sore pressed, and to his help Sir Archie went. The fresh assault made the seaman duck his head, and Dougal seized the occasion to smite him hard with something which caused him to roll over. It was Spidel’s life-preserver which he had annexed that afternoon.

===Chinese===
突然，杜加尔的哨子响了，紧接着，西面什么地方传来山崩地裂的一声巨响。阿奇爵士喊一声“守稳，麦古福格”，拔腿冲进大厅，循声奔到那间原先是女眷卧室的屋子。眼前一幅怪景：一扇窗的整个框架像是被齐根推了进来，豁口当中，亚历克西斯抡着一只炉挡。屋里已有三名敌人——一个昏死在地上，一个落在西姆手里，被他那只独手死死掐住喉咙，还有一个正在墙角同杜加尔缠斗。死硬帮头领岌岌可危，阿奇爵士立时赶去接应。新添的攻势唬得那水手一缩头，杜加尔趁势抡起一件家伙狠狠一下，把他打得就地翻滚。那正是斯皮德尔的灌铅短棍，他当天下午刚刚缴获的战利品。

===Original===
Alexis at the window seemed to have for a moment daunted the attack. “Bring that table,” he cried, and the thing was jammed into the gap. “Now you”﻿—this to Sime﻿—“get the man from the back door to hold this place with his gun. There’s no attack there. It’s about time for shooting now, or we’ll have them in our rear. What in heaven is that?”

===Chinese===
窗边的亚历克西斯似乎一时间慑住了攻势。“把那张桌子搬来。”他喊道，桌子随即死死楔进了豁口。“现在你”——这是对西姆说——“叫后门那个人带上枪来守这儿。后门眼下没动静。该到开枪的时候了，不然他们要抄到咱们背后去了。天哪，那是什么？”

===Original===
It was McGuffog whose great bellow resounded down the corridor. Sir Archie turned and shuffled back, to be met by a distressing spectacle. The lamp, burning as peacefully as it might have burned on an old lady’s tea-table, revealed the window of the garden-room driven bodily inward, shutters and all, and now forming an inclined bridge over Dougal’s ineffectual tubs. In front of it stood McGuffog, swinging his gun by the barrel and yelling curses, which, being mainly couched in the vernacular, were happily meaningless to Saskia. She herself stood at the hall door, plucking at something hidden in her breast. He saw that it was a little ivory-handled pistol.

===Chinese===
是麦古福格，他的大吼顺着走廊轰隆隆滚过来。阿奇爵士转身一路踉跄往回赶，迎面撞上一幅揪心的景象。那盏灯烧得安安静静，仿佛亮在哪位老太太茶桌上的一盏，灯下，花园厅的整扇窗连百叶带框被整个砸进屋来，正斜搭在杜加尔那些没顶上事的水桶上，成了一道斜桥。麦古福格立在窗前，拎着枪管抡枪，扯开嗓子破口大骂，好在骂的主要是苏格兰土话，萨斯基亚听来全然不知所云。她本人立在大厅门口，正往怀里摸索什么。他看清了——那是一支象牙柄的小手枪。

===Original===
The enemy’s feint had succeeded, for even as Sir Archie looked three men leaped into the room. On the neck of one the butt of McGuffog’s gun crashed, but two scrambled to their feet and made for the girl. Sir Archie met the first with his fist, a clean drive on the jaw, followed by a damaging hook with his left that put him out of action. The other hesitated for an instant and was lost, for McGuffog caught him by the waist from behind and sent him through the broken frame to join his comrades without.

===Chinese===
敌人的佯攻得手了，就在阿奇爵士眼皮底下，三条汉子跃进屋来。麦古福格的枪托砸上其中一人的脖颈，另外两个爬起身，直扑那姑娘。阿奇爵士迎住头一个，一拳端端正正打在下巴上，跟上左手一记要命的勾拳，叫他就此歇了菜。第二个迟疑了一瞬，也就误在那一瞬——麦古福格从背后拦腰抱住，把他从破窗框里掼出去，送他到外头与同伙作伴。

===Original===
“Up the stairs,” Dougal was shouting, for the little room beyond the hall was clearly impossible. “Our flank’s turned. They’re pourin’ through the other windy.” Out of a corner of his eye Sir Archie caught sight of Alexis, with Sime and Carfrae in support, being slowly forced towards them along the corridor. “Upstairs,” he shouted. “Come on, McGuffog. Lead on, Princess.” He dashed out the lamp, and the place was in darkness.

===Chinese===
“上楼！”杜加尔在高喊，大厅那头的小屋显然是守不住了。“咱们的侧翼叫人抄了。他们正从另一扇窗往里灌。”阿奇爵士眼角一瞥，望见亚历克西斯在西姆和卡弗雷的接应下，正沿着走廊被一步一步逼向他们。“上楼！”他喊道。“跟上，麦古福格。公主，你领头。”他一把扑灭灯，四下登时坠入黑暗。

===Original===
With this retreat from the forward trench line ended the opening phase of the battle. It was achieved in good order, and position was taken up on the first-floor landing, dominating the main staircase and the passage that led to the back stairs. At their back was a short corridor ending in a window which gave on the north side of the House above the verandah, and from which an active man might descend to the verandah roof. It had been carefully reconnoitred beforehand by Dougal, and his were the dispositions.

===Chinese===
随着这次自前沿战壕的撤退，战斗的第一阶段落下帷幕。撤退完成得秩序井然，队伍在二楼楼梯平台占据了阵地，俯制着主楼梯和通向后楼梯的过道。他们背后是一条短走廊，尽头一扇窗，开在游廊上方的宅子北面，身手利落的人可以从那里下到游廊顶上。这一带杜加尔事先仔细侦察过，部署也全出自他的手笔。

===Original===
The odd thing was that the retreating force were in good heart. The three men from the Mains were warming to their work, and McGuffog wore an air of genial ferocity. “Dashed fine position I call this,” said Sir Archie. Only Alexis was silent and preoccupied. “We are still at their mercy,” he said. “Pray God your police come soon.” He forbade shooting yet awhile. “The lady is our strong card,” he said. “They won’t use their guns while she is with us, but if it ever comes to shooting they can wipe us out in a couple of minutes. One of you watch that window, for Paul Abreskov is no fool.”

===Chinese===
奇怪的是，撤退下来的这支队伍士气高昂。田庄来的三条汉子越干越起劲，麦古福格更是一脸和气的凶相。“要我说，这位置真是没治了。”阿奇爵士道。只有亚历克西斯沉默着，心事重重。“我们仍在人家掌心里，”他说，“但愿上帝保佑，你们的警察快些到。”他暂时仍不许开枪。“这位夫人是我们的王牌，”他说。“她同我们在一起，他们就不敢用枪；可一旦真开了枪，他们两分钟就能把我们斩尽杀绝。你们当中得有一人盯住那扇窗，保罗·阿布列斯科夫可不是好糊弄的。”

===Original===
Their exhilaration was short-lived. Below in the hall it was black darkness save for a greyness at the entrance of the verandah passage; but the defence was soon aware that the place was thick with men. Presently there came a scuffling from Carfrae’s post towards the back stairs, and a cry as of someone choking. And at the same moment a flare was lit below which brought the whole hall from floor to rafters into blinding light.

===Chinese===
他们的兴头没能撑多久。楼下大厅里一团漆黑，只有游廊过道口浮着一线灰白；可守军很快便察觉，那底下已是人烟稠密。不多时，卡弗雷的岗位——后楼梯那头——传来一阵扭打声，还有一声像人被扼住喉咙的闷叫。也就在同一刻，楼下一支照明火把点起，整座大厅从地面到椽子，全被罩进一片炫目的强光里。

===Original===
It revealed a crowd of figures, some still in the hall and some halfway up the stairs, and it revealed, too, more figures at the end of the upper landing where Carfrae had been stationed. The shapes were motionless like mannequins in a shop window.

===Chinese===
火光照出黑压压的一群人影，有的还立在大厅里，有的已上半截楼梯；火光还照出，二楼平台尽头——卡弗雷受命驻守的那头——也有几条人影。那些身形纹丝不动，像橱窗里的人体模型。

===Original===
“They’ve got us treed all right,” Sir Archie groaned. “What the devil are they waiting for?”

===Chinese===
“咱们这回真叫人家赶上树了。”阿奇爵士呻吟道。“他们究竟在等什么鬼名堂？”

===Original===
“They wait for their leader,” said Alexis.

===Chinese===
“他们在等他们的首领。”亚历克西斯道。

===Original===
No one of the party will ever forget the ensuing minutes. After the hubbub of the barricades the ominous silence was like icy water, chilling and petrifying with an indefinable fear. There was no sound but the wind, but presently mingled with it came odd wild voices.

===Chinese===
这一行人中，再没有谁忘得了随后的那几分钟。路障那场喧嚣过后，这杀气腾腾的寂静就像一潭冰水，浸得人透骨地冷，凝起一股说不出的恐惧。四下里只有风声，可渐渐地，风声里掺进了几缕古怪的野音。

===Original===
“Hear to the whaups,” McGuffog whispered.

===Chinese===
“听听，杓鹬（whaups）叫呢。”麦古福格悄声道。

===Original===
Sir Archie, who found the tension unbearable, sought relief in contradiction. “You’re an unscientific brute, McGuffog,” he told his henchman. “It’s a disgrace that a gamekeeper should be such a rotten naturalist. What would whaups be doin’ here at this time of year?”

===Chinese===
阿奇爵士被这份紧绷绷得再也受不了，便靠抬杠来松快松快。“麦古福格，你这家伙全无科学头脑，”他对自己的部下说。“一个猎场看守，博物学竟烂成这样，真是丢人。这种时节，杓鹬跑到这里来干什么？”

===Original===
“A’ the same, I could swear it’s whaups, Sir Erchibald.”

===Chinese===
“随您怎么说，俺敢起誓，那就是杓鹬，埃尔奇爵士。”

===Original===
Then Dougal broke in and his voice was excited. “It’s no whaups. That’s our patrol signal. Man, there’s hope for us yet. I believe it’s the polis.”

===Chinese===
这时杜加尔插了进来，嗓音透着兴奋。“才不是什么杓鹬。那是咱们的巡逻暗号。伙计，咱们还有指望。俺琢磨着，是警察到了。”

===Original===
His words were unheeded, for the figures below drew apart and a young man came through them. His beautifully-shaped dark head was bare, and as he moved he unbuttoned his oilskins and showed the trim dark-blue garb of the yachtsman. He walked confidently up the stairs, an odd elegant figure among his heavy companions.

===Chinese===
他的话没人理会，因为底下的人影分开来，一个青年从中间穿行而出。他那一头乌发轮廓俊美，头上不戴帽子，边走边解开油布雨衣，露出一身利落的藏青色游艇装。他自信满满地拾级而上，在一群粗壮的同伴中间，是个格格不入的雅致身影。

===Original===
“Good afternoon, Alexis,” he said in English. “I think we may now regard this interesting episode as closed. I take it that you surrender. Saskia, dear, you are coming with me on a little journey. Will you tell my men where to find your baggage?”

===Chinese===
“午安，亚历克西斯。”他用英语说道。“我想，这出有趣的插曲，如今可以落幕了。我就当你们投降了。亲爱的萨斯基亚，你这就随我来，作一次小小的旅行。请吩咐我的人，到哪儿去找你的行李？”

===Original===
The reply was in Russian. Alexis’ voice was as cool as the other’s, and it seemed to wake him to anger. He replied in a rapid torrent of words, and appealed to the men below, who shouted back. The flare was dying down, and shadows again hid most of the hall.

===Chinese===
回话用的是俄语。亚历克西斯的嗓音同对方一般冷，那冷倒像是把他激恼了。他连珠炮似的回敬了一长串，又向底下的人发出呼吁，人群轰然应和。火把渐渐衰弱下去，暗影重新罩没了大半座大厅。

===Original===
Dougal crept up behind Sir Archie. “Here, I think it’s the polis. They’re whistlin’ outbye, and I hear folk cryin’ to each other﻿—no’ the foreigners.”

===Chinese===
杜加尔悄悄蹭到阿奇爵士背后。“喂，俺看是警察。外头有哨子响，俺还听见有人彼此喊话——不是那帮外国佬。”

===Original===
Again Alexis spoke, and then Saskia joined in. What she said rang sharp with contempt, and her fingers played with her little pistol.

===Chinese===
亚历克西斯再度开口，随后萨斯基亚也加入进来。她的话锋利如刀，声声是鄙夷，手指还摩挲着那支小手枪。

===Original===
Suddenly before the young man could answer Dobson bustled towards him. The innkeeper was labouring under some strong emotion, for he seemed to be pleading and pointing urgently towards the door.

===Chinese===
青年还没来得及答话，多布森忽然急匆匆朝他奔来。这位客栈老板显然被一股强烈的情绪支使着，只见他似在苦苦哀求，又急不可待地朝大门直指。

===Original===
“I tell ye it’s the polis,” whispered Dougal. “They’re nickit.”

===Chinese===
“俺跟你说，是警察。”杜加尔咬耳朵道。“他们栽定了。”

===Original===
There was a swaying in the crowd and anxious faces. Men surged in, whispered and went out, and a clamour arose which the leader stilled with a fierce gesture.

===Chinese===
人群晃动起来，一张张脸上写满惶急。有人涌进来，交头接耳，又奔出去；喧声骤然而起，又被头领一记凶狠的手势压了下去。

===Original===
“You there,” he cried, looking up, “you English. We mean you no ill, but I require you to hand over to me the lady and the Russian who is with her. I give you a minute by my watch to decide. If you refuse my men are behind you and around you, and you go with me to be punished at my leisure.”

===Chinese===
“上头的，听着，”他仰面喊道，“你们这些英国人。我们无意伤害你们，但我要求你们把那位夫人，连同陪她的俄国人，交到我手上。我看着表，限你们一分钟作出决断。你们若是拒绝——我的人就在你们身后、四下包围，你们将随我同去，由我得空慢慢发落。”

===Original===
“I warn you,” cried Sir Archie. “We are armed, and will shoot down anyone who dares to lay a hand on us.”

===Chinese===
“我警告你，”阿奇爵士喊道。“我们手里有枪，谁敢对我们动一根指头，我们就撂倒谁。”

===Original===
“You fool,” came the answer. “I can send you all to eternity before you touch a trigger.”

===Chinese===
“你这蠢货，”对方答道。“你们的指头还没碰着扳机，我就能把你们全体送进永恒。”

===Original===
Léon was by his side now﻿—Léon and Spidel, imploring him to do something which he angrily refused. Outside there was a new clamour, faces showing at the door and then vanishing, and an anxious hum filled the hall.﻿ ﻿… Dobson appeared again and this time he was a figure of fury.

===Chinese===
这时莱昂已到了他身旁——莱昂和斯皮德尔，苦苦求他做一件什么事，被他怒冲冲地拒绝了。外头又起了一阵新的骚动，门口一晃晃过几张人脸，随即不见；大厅里嗡嗡地涨满了不安的声浪。……多布森再度现身，这一回，他整个人成了怒火铸的。

===Original===
“Are ye daft, man?” he cried. “I tell ye the polis are closin’ round us, and there’s no’ a moment to lose if we would get back to the boats. If ye’ll no’ think o’ your own neck, I’m thinkin’ o’ mine. The whole thing’s a bloody misfire. Come on, lads, if ye’re no’ besotted on destruction.”

===Chinese===
“你疯了吗，伙计？”他嚷道。“俺告诉你，警察正围拢上来，想赶回小艇去，一刻也耽搁不得了。你不心疼自己那条脖子，俺还心疼俺这条呢。这整档子事，血本无归，全砸了。走吧，弟兄们，只要你们还没昏了头，一门心思找死。”

===Original===
Léon laid a hand on the leader’s arm and was roughly shaken off. Spidel fared no better, and the little group on the upper landing saw the two shrug their shoulders and make for the door. The hall was emptying fast, and the watchers had gone from the back stairs. The young man’s voice rose to a scream; he commanded, threatened, cursed; but panic was in the air and he had lost his mastery.

===Chinese===
莱昂把一只手搭上头领的臂膀，被粗暴地甩开。斯皮德尔去劝，也没讨着好；二楼平台上的这一小簇人，眼看着那两位耸了耸肩，径直往大门去了。大厅飞快地空下来，后楼梯上的望风人也撤了。青年的嗓音拔成了尖叫；他下令、恫吓、咒骂；可恐慌已经弥漫在空气里，他号令不动了。

===Original===
“Quick,” croaked Dougal, “now’s the time for the counterattack.”

===Chinese===
“快，”杜加尔哑声催促，“反攻的时机到了。”

===Original===
But the figure on the stairs held them motionless. They could not see his face, but by instinct they knew that it was distraught with fury and defeat. The flare blazed up again as the flame caught a knot of fresh powder, and once more the place was bright with the uncanny light.﻿ ﻿… The hall was empty save for the pale man who was in the act of turning.

===Chinese===
可楼梯上那道身影，慑得众人一动不能动。他们看不清他的脸，但凭本能知道，那张脸已被狂怒与败局绞得变了形。火苗舔着一撮新火药，火光轰地重又窜起，大厅再度浸在那片鬼气森森的亮光里。……大厅空了，只剩那面色苍白的男人，正要抽身而去。

===Original===
He looked back. “If I go now, I will return. The world is not wide enough to hide you from me, Saskia.”

===Chinese===
他回头望了一眼。“我今日即便走了，也必再来。这世界还不够宽广，藏不下你，萨斯基亚。”

===Original===
“You will never get her,” said Alexis.

===Chinese===
“你永远得不到她。”亚历克西斯道。

===Original===
A sudden devil flamed into his eyes, the devil of some ancestral savagery, which would destroy what is desired but unattainable. He swung round, his hand went to his pocket, something clicked, and his arm shot out like a baseball pitcher’s.

===Chinese===
一团凶焰骤然蹿进他眼中——那是某种祖传的兽性之魔，专要毁掉求而不得的东西。他猛地转身，手插进口袋，什么东西咔哒一响，手臂像棒球投手般疾掷而出。

===Original===
So intent was the gaze of the others on him, that they did not see a second figure ascending the stairs. Just as Alexis flung himself before the Princess, the newcomer caught the young man’s outstretched arm and wrenched something from his hand. The next second he had hurled it into a far corner where stood the great fireplace. There was a blinding sheet of flame, a dull roar, and then billow upon billow of acrid smoke. As it cleared they saw that the fine Italian chimneypiece, the pride of the builder of the House, was a mass of splinters, and that a great hole had been blown through the wall into what had been the dining-room.﻿ ﻿… A figure was sitting on the bottom step feeling its bruises. The last enemy had gone.

===Chinese===
众人的目光全钉在他身上，谁也没看见第二道身影正拾级而上。就在亚历克西斯抢身挡在公主面前的当口，那新来的汉子一把擎住青年伸出的手臂，从他手里硬夺下一件东西。说时迟那时快，他把那玩意儿远远掼进墙角那座大壁炉里。眼前炸开一片炫目的火光，闷雷般一声轰响，随即浓烟一阵接着一阵，呛得人睁不开眼。烟散处，众人看见那座精美的意大利式壁炉台——建宅人的心头骄傲——已成了一堆碎木，墙上炸穿一个大窟窿，直通从前做餐厅的那间屋子。……一道身影坐在最底下一级台阶上，正揉着身上的青肿。最后一个敌人去了。

===Original===
When Mr. John Heritage raised his eyes he saw the Princess with a very pale face in the arms of a tall man whom he had never seen before. If he was surprised at the sight, he did not show it. “Nasty little bomb that. Time fuse. I remember we struck the brand first in July ’18.”

===Chinese===
约翰·赫里蒂奇先生抬起眼来，看见公主脸色惨白，偎在一个他从未见过的高个子男人怀里。这一幕纵然令他诧异，他也没有露出来。“那颗小炸弹够损的。定时引信。记得咱们头一回撞上这牌子货，还在’18——7月里。”

===Original===
“Are they rounded up?” Sir Archie asked.

===Chinese===
“他们一网打尽了吗？”阿奇爵士问。

===Original===
“They’ve bolted. Whether they’ll get away is another matter. I left half the mounted police a minute ago at the top of the West Lodge avenue. The other lot went to the Garplefoot to cut off the boats.”

===Chinese===
“他们跑了。跑不跑得掉，另说。一分钟前，我刚把半队骑警留在西门房林荫道的顶头。另外那半队去了加普尔河下游，截他们的小艇。”

===Original===
“Good Lord, man,” Sir Archie cried, “the police have been here for the last ten minutes.”

===Chinese===
“老天爷，老兄，”阿奇爵士喊道，“警察十分钟前就到了。”

===Original===
“You’re wrong. They came with me.”

===Chinese===
“你弄错了。他们是跟我一道来的。”

===Original===
“Then what on earth﻿—?” began the astonished baronet. He stopped short, for he suddenly got his answer. Into the hall from the verandah limped a boy. Never was there seen so ruinous a child. He was dripping wet, his shirt was all but torn off his back, his bleeding nose was poorly staunched by a wisp of handkerchief, his breeches were in ribbons, and his poor bare legs looked as if they had been comprehensively kicked and scratched. Limpingly he entered, yet with a kind of pride, like some small cock-sparrow who has lost most of his plumage but has vanquished his adversary.

===Chinese===
“那到底是怎么——？”惊愕的从男爵才起了个头。他猛地顿住，因为答案不请自来。一个男孩从游廊一瘸一拐走进了大厅。天下再没见过这么一副狼狈相的孩子。他浑身淌水，衬衫差不多从背上撕了个精光，流血的鼻孔里胡乱塞着一小绺手帕，马裤成了破布条，两条可怜的光腿一望便知，挨遍了拳脚、抓满了道道血痕。他一瘸一拐地走进来，却自有一股骄傲，像一只掉了大半身羽毛、却斗胜了对头的小公雀。

===Original===
With a yell Dougal went down the stairs. The boy saluted him, and they gravely shook hands. It was the meeting of Wellington and Blücher.

===Chinese===
杜加尔一声欢呼，冲下楼去。男孩向他敬礼，两人郑重其事地握了握手。这是惠灵顿（Wellington）与布吕歇尔（Blücher）的会师。

===Original===
The Chieftain’s voice shrilled in triumph, but there was a break in it. The glory was almost too great to be borne.

===Chinese===
首领的嗓音尖亮，满是得胜的欢腾，内里却带着一丝哽咽。这份荣耀，大得几乎承受不住。

===Original===
“I kenned it,” he cried. “It was the Gorbals Die-Hards. There stands the man that done it.﻿ ﻿… Ye’ll no’ fickle Thomas Yownie.”

===Chinese===
“俺就知道！”他喊道。“是戈尔巴尔死硬帮干的！立下这功的，就站在那儿。……谁也别想糊弄托马斯·尤尼！”

## XV. The Gorbals Die-Hards Go Into Action / 十五、戈尔巴尔死硬帮出击
===Original===
We left Mr. McCunn, full of aches but desperately resolute in spirit, hobbling by the Auchenlochan road into the village of Dalquharter. His goal was Mrs. Morran’s henhouse, which was Thomas Yownie’s *poste de commandement*. The rain had come on again, and, though in other weather there would have been a slow twilight, already the shadow of night had the world in its grip. The sea even from the high ground was invisible, and all to westward and windward was a ragged screen of dark cloud. It was foul weather for foul deeds.

===Chinese===
我们把麦坎先生撇在了半路：他浑身酸痛，心气却决绝得很，正一瘸一拐顺着奥琴洛坎大路走进达尔夸特村。他的目标是莫兰太太家的鸡棚，托马斯·尤尼的*司令部*（poste de commandement）便设在那儿。雨又下了起来，换作平常天气，暮色本该缓缓降临，如今夜影却已一把攥住了世界。即便从高地上望，海也看不见，整个西面、整个上风头，是一幅破破烂烂的黑云屏障。这天气，正好配得上肮脏的勾当。

===Original===
Thomas Yownie was not in the henhouse, but in Mrs. Morran’s kitchen, and with him were the pug-faced boy known as Old Bill, and the sturdy figure of Peter Paterson. But the floor was held by the hostess. She still wore her big boots, her petticoats were still kilted, and round her venerable head in lieu of a bonnet was drawn a tartan shawl.

===Chinese===
托马斯·尤尼不在鸡棚里，而在莫兰太太的厨房里，同他在一起的，是那个长着一张哈巴狗脸、人称老比尔的孩子，还有彼得·帕特森那敦实的身板。但厨房这块阵地握在女主人手里。她脚上还蹬着大靴子，衬裙还高高掖在腰间，那颗令人起敬的脑袋上没戴帽子，只裹了一条格子呢披肩。

===Original===
“Eh, Dickson, but I’m blithe to see ye. And, puir man, ye’ve been sair mishandled. This is the awfu’est Sabbath day that ever you and me pit in. I hope it’ll be forgiven us.﻿ ﻿… Whaur’s the young leddy?”

===Chinese===
“哎哟，迪克森，俺可真高兴见着你。可怜见的，你叫他们糟践得不轻。这是你我俩一辈子过的最邪门的安息日，但愿上天肯饶恕咱们。……年轻的小姐在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Dougal was saying she was in the House with Sir Archibald and the men from the Mains.”

===Chinese===
“杜加尔说，她同阿奇博尔德爵士，还有田庄来的人，都在大宅里。”

===Original===
“Wae’s me!” Mrs. Morran keened. “And what kind o’ place is yon for her? Thae laddies tell me there’s boatfu’s o’ scoondrels landit at the Garplefit. They’ll try the auld Tower, but they’ll no’ wait there when they find it toom, and they’ll be inside the Hoose in a jiffy and awa’ wi’ the puir lassie. Sirs, it maunna be. Ye’re lippenin’ to the polis, but in a’ my days I never kenned the polis in time. We maun be up and daein’ oorsels. Oh, if I could get a haud o’ that red-heided Dougal.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“苦了俺了！”莫兰太太哀声道。“那地方哪是她待的？这帮小子告诉俺，加普尔河下游上了岸一船一船的恶棍。他们会去打老塔的主意，可一见塔里空了，他们不会在那儿耽搁，转眼就要闯进府里，把可怜的姑娘掳走。天爷，这可使不得。你们指望警察，可俺活这些年，就没见过警察赶趟过。咱们得自己起来干。哎，要是俺能逮住那个红头发的杜加尔。……”

===Original===
As she spoke, there came on the wind the dull reverberation of an explosion.

===Chinese===
正说着，风里传来一声爆炸的沉闷回响。

===Original===
“Keep us, what’s that?” she cried.

===Chinese===
“老天保佑，那是什么？”她叫道。

===Original===
“It’s dinnymite,” said Peter Paterson.

===Chinese===
“是炸药，”彼得·帕特森说。

===Original===
“That’s the end o’ the auld Tower,” observed Thomas Yownie in his quiet even voice. “And it’s likely the end o’ the man Heritage.”

===Chinese===
“老塔这就完了，”托马斯·尤尼用他那平静匀净的嗓音评说道。“多半也完了的，还有赫里蒂奇先生那个人。”

===Original===
“Lord peety us!” the old woman wailed. “And us standin’ here like stookies and no’ liftin’ a hand. Awa’ wi’ ye, laddies, and dae something. Awa’ you too, Dickson, or I’ll tak’ the road mysel’.”

===Chinese===
“主啊，可怜可怜俺们！”老妇人哀号道。“俺们倒好，站在这儿一根根木头桩子似的，连把手也不抬。去吧，小子们，干点正经的去。你也去，迪克森，不然俺自个儿就上路了。”

===Original===
“I’ve got orders,” said the Chief of Staff, “no’ to move till the sityation’s clear. Napoleon’s up at the Tower and Jaikie in the policies. I maun wait on their reports.”

===Chinese===
“俺奉了命令，”参谋长说，“局势没弄清之前，一步也不许动。拿破仑在塔那边，杰基在园地里。俺得等他们的报告。”

===Original===
For a moment Mrs. Morran’s attention was distracted by Dickson, who suddenly felt very faint and sat down heavily on a kitchen chair. “Man, ye’re as white as a dishclout,” she exclaimed with compunction. “Ye’re fair wore out, and ye’ll have had nae meat sin’ your breakfast. See, and I’ll get ye a cup o’ tea.”

===Chinese===
有那么一刻，莫兰太太的注意力让迪克森岔开了——他突然觉得天旋地转，一屁股沉甸甸坐倒在厨房椅子上。“伙计，你白得像块抹碗布，”她心疼地叫起来。“你真是累垮了，打早饭起就没进过一口饭食吧。瞧着，俺这就给你倒杯茶。”

===Original===
She proved to be in the right, for as soon as Dickson had swallowed some mouthfuls of her strong scalding brew the colour came back to his cheeks, and he announced that he felt better. “Ye’ll fortify it wi’ a dram,” she told him, and produced a black bottle from her cupboard. “My father aye said that guid whiskey and het tea keepit the doctor’s gig oot o’ the close.”

===Chinese===
她这话说着了，迪克森几口她那又浓又烫的酽茶下肚，脸颊上便回了血色，自称觉得好些了。“你得往里添一盅酒，壮一壮，”她叮嘱着，从碗柜里摸出一只黑瓶子。“俺爹在时常说，好威士忌加热茶，保得医生的双轮马车进不了咱巷口。”

===Original===
The back door opened and Napoleon entered, his thin shanks blue with cold. He saluted and made his report in a voice shrill with excitement.

===Chinese===
后门开了，拿破仑走进来，两条细腿冻得发青。他敬了个礼，用激动得发尖的嗓音作报告。

===Original===
“The Tower has fallen. They’ve blown in the big door, and the feck o’ them’s inside.”

===Chinese===
“塔丢了。他们炸开了大门，大半都钻进去了。”

===Original===
“And Mr. Heritage?” was Dickson’s anxious inquiry.

===Chinese===
“赫里蒂奇先生呢？”迪克森急切地打听。

===Original===
“When I last saw him he was up at a windy, shootin’. I think he’s gotten on to the roof. I wouldna wonder but the place is on fire.”

===Chinese===
“俺末了瞅见他，他正占着窗口打枪。俺琢磨他上房顶了。那地方八成着起来了。”

===Original===
“Here, this is awful,” Dickson groaned. “We can’t let Mr. Heritage be killed that way. What strength is the enemy?”

===Chinese===
“哎呀，这可糟透了，”迪克森呻吟道。“总不能眼看着赫里蒂奇先生就这么送命。敌人有多少人？”

===Original===
“I counted twenty-seven, and there’s stragglers comin’ up from the boats.”

===Chinese===
“俺数了，二十七个，还有零散的正从船上跟上来。”

===Original===
“And there’s me and you five laddies here, and Dougal and the others shut up in the House.” He stopped in sheer despair. It was a fix from which the most enlightened business mind showed no escape. Prudence, inventiveness were no longer in question; only some desperate course of violence.

===Chinese===
“可咱们手头，只有俺和你，加五个小子，杜加尔他们又关在大宅里。”他彻底绝望，说不下去了。这局面，再开明的生意头脑也寻不出一条活路。稳妥与巧计都已无从谈起，只剩某种孤注一掷的暴力一途。

===Original===
“We must create a diversion,” he said. “I’m for the Tower, and you laddies must come with me. We’ll maybe see a chance. Oh, but I wish I had my wee pistol.”

===Chinese===
“咱们得牵制一下敌人，”他说。“俺这就去塔那边，你们小子跟俺来。兴许能撞上个机会。唉，俺那支小手枪要还在身上就好了。”

===Original===
“If ye’re gaun there, Dickson, I’m comin’ wi’ ye,” Mrs. Morran announced.

===Chinese===
“你要上那儿去，迪克森，俺就跟你一道，”莫兰太太宣布。

===Original===
Her words revealed to Dickson the preposterousness of the whole situation, and for all his anxiety he laughed. “Five laddies, a middle-aged man and an auld wife,” he cried. “Dod, it’s pretty hopeless. It’s like the thing in the Bible about the weak things of the world trying to confound the strong.”

===Chinese===
她这一句话，叫迪克森看清了整个局面的荒唐透顶，他焦心归焦心，还是笑了。“五个小子，一个半老的人，一个上了年纪的老婆子，”他嚷道。“老天，实在没大指望。活像《圣经》里讲的，世上软弱的物，偏要去制伏那强壮的。”

===Original===
“The Bible’s whiles richt,” Mrs. Morran answered drily. “Come on, for there’s no time to lose.”

===Chinese===
“《圣经》有时候就说得准，”莫兰太太干巴巴地答道。“走吧，一分钟也耽误不得。”

===Original===
The door opened again to admit the figure of Wee Jaikie. There were no tears in his eyes, and his face was very white.

===Chinese===
门又开了，进来的是小杰基的身影。他眼里没有泪，脸白得吓人。

===Original===
“They’re a’ round the Hoose,” he croaked. “I was up a tree forenent the verandy and seen them. The lassie ran oot and cried on them from the top o’ the brae, and they a’ turned and hunted her back. Gosh, but it was a near thing. I seen the Captain sklimmin’ the wall, and a muckle man took the lassie and flung her up the ladder. They got inside just in time and steekit the door, and now the whole pack is roarin’ round the Hoose seekin’ a road in. They’ll no’ be long over the job, neither.”

===Chinese===
“他们把府围了个严实，”他哑着嗓子说。“俺趴在游廊对面的树上，全瞧见了。姑娘跑出去，在坡顶上朝他们喊，他们一窝蜂转过身，把她撵了回去。好家伙，就差那么一丝丝。俺瞧见上尉翻墙，一个大个子抓起姑娘，把她甩上了梯子。他们进门刚刚来得及，就闩死了门，这会儿那一整群人正围着府吼，寻摸着往里闯的路。这活儿他们费不了多久工夫。”

===Original===
“What about Mr. Heritage?”

===Chinese===
“赫里蒂奇先生怎么样了？”

===Original===
“They’re no’ heedin’ about him any more. The auld Tower’s bleezin’.”

===Chinese===
“他们顾不上他喽。老塔烧起来了。”

===Original===
“Worse and worse,” said Dickson. “If the police don’t come in the next ten minutes, they’ll be away with the Princess. They’ve beaten all Dougal’s plans, and it’s a straight fight with odds of six to one. It’s not possible.”

===Chinese===
“越闹越糟，”迪克森说。“警察十分钟里再不到，他们就要带着公主跑喽。杜加尔的计划全让他们破了，如今是一场硬碰硬，一比六的悬殊仗。这撑不住的。”

===Original===
Mrs. Morran for the first time seemed to lose hope. “Eh, the puir lassie!” she wailed, and sinking on a chair covered her face with her shawl.

===Chinese===
莫兰太太头一回像是失了指望。“哎，可怜的姑娘！”她哀声道，颓然坐倒在椅子上，拿披肩蒙住了脸。

===Original===
“Laddies, can you no’ think of a plan?” asked Dickson, his voice flat with despair.

===Chinese===
“小子们，你们就想不出个法子来？”迪克森问，嗓音让绝望压得平平的。

===Original===
Then Thomas Yownie spoke. So far he had been silent, but under his tangled thatch of hair, his mind had been busy. Jaikie’s report seemed to bring him to a decision.

===Chinese===
这时托马斯·尤尼开了口。此前他一直没作声，可那蓬乱如茅草的头发底下，脑子一直没闲着。杰基的报告像是促成了他的决断。

===Original===
“It’s gey dark,” he said, “and it’s gettin’ darker.”

===Chinese===
“天黑得够呛，”他说，“而且越来越黑。”

===Original===
There was that in his voice which promised something, and Dickson listened.

===Chinese===
他话音里有种要拿出名堂来的意味，迪克森凝神听着。

===Original===
“The enemy’s mostly foreigners, but Dobson’s there and I think he’s a kind of guide to them. Dobson’s feared of the polis, and if we can terrify Dobson he’ll terrify the rest.”

===Chinese===
“敌人多半是外国人，可多布森在他们堆里，俺看他是充当向导的角色。多布森怕警察，咱们只要吓破多布森的胆，他自会替咱们吓破其余人的胆。”

===Original===
“Ay, but where are the police?”

===Chinese===
“是啊，可警察这会儿在哪儿呢？”

===Original===
“They’re no’ here yet, but they’re comin’. The fear o’ them is aye in Dobson’s mind. If he thinks the polis has arrived, he’ll put the wind up the lot.﻿ ﻿… *We* maun be the polis.”

===Chinese===
“人还没到，可正在路上。那份怕，一直盘在多布森心里。他只要认定警察到了，就能把一整伙人吓个屁滚尿流。……得*咱们*来当这警察。”

===Original===
Dickson could only stare while the Chief of Staff unfolded his scheme. I do not know to whom the Muse of History will give the credit of the tactics of “infiltration”﻿—whether to Ludendorff or von Hutier or some other proud captain of Germany, or to Foch, who revised and perfected them. But I know that the same notion was at this moment of crisis conceived by Thomas Yownie, whom parents acknowledged, who slept usually in a coal cellar, and who had picked up his education among Gorbals closes and along the wharves of Clyde.

===Chinese===
参谋长把他的方略一五一十摆出来，迪克森只有瞪眼听的份儿。“渗透”战术这份功劳，历史的缪斯女神日后会记到谁头上，我不得而知——是记给鲁登道夫（Ludendorff），还是冯·胡蒂尔（von Hutier），还是德意志哪一位志骄意满的统帅，抑或是那位把它修订完善的福煦（Foch）。但我知道，同样的念头，正是在这个危局的当口，由托马斯·尤尼想了出来——此人有爹娘认领，平日睡在煤窖里，他那一肚子学问，是从戈尔巴尔的穷街陋巷和克莱德河的码头边上捡来的。

===Original===
“It’s gettin’ dark,” he said, “and the enemy are that busy tryin’ to break into the Hoose that they’ll no’ be thinkin’ o’ their rear. The five o’ us Die-Hards is grand at dodgin’ and keepin’ out of sight, and what hinders us to get in among them, so that they’ll hear us but never see us? We’re used to the ways o’ the polis, and can imitate them fine. Forbye we’ve all got our whistles, which are the same as a bobbie’s birl, and Old Bill and Peter are grand at copyin’ a man’s voice. Since the Captain is shut up in the Hoose, the command falls to me, and that’s my plan.”

===Chinese===
“天黑了，”他说，“敌人正一门心思往府里闯，顾不上想想自家屁股后头。俺们死硬帮这五个，闪转腾挪、藏形匿影是拿手好戏，有啥拦着俺们钻进他们当中，叫他们只闻其声、不见其人？警察那套做派俺们熟得很，学起来像模像样。再说俺们人人兜里揣着哨子，跟巡警的警笛一个动静，老比尔和彼得学人腔调，学得能瞒过亲娘。上尉既然关在府里，指挥就落到俺头上，俺的打算就是这样。”

===Original===
With a piece of chalk he drew on the kitchen floor a rough sketch of the environs of Huntingtower. Peter Paterson was to move from the shrubberies beyond the verandah, Napoleon from the stables, Old Bill from the Tower, while Wee Jaikie and Thomas himself were to advance as if from the Garplefoot, so that the enemy might fear for his communications. “As soon as one o’ ye gets into position he’s to gie the patrol cry, and when each o’ ye has heard five cries, he’s to advance. Begin birlin’ and roarin’ afore ye get among them, and keep it up till ye’re at the Hoose wall. If they’ve gotten inside, in ye go after them. I trust each Die-Hard to use his judgment, and above all to keep out o’ sight and no let himsel’ be grippit.”

===Chinese===
